- [x] **Step 7: Implement — create `src/Lod.Core/Common/GeometryLimits.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System;

namespace Lod.Core.Common;

/// <summary>
/// The single place where the minimum wall and window geometry of plan generators is defined (ADR-011; GLOBAL.md, quality rule 4). An outdoor wall
/// below <see cref="MinimumWindowWallLength"/>, or a window below <see cref="MinimumWindowWidth"/> or <see cref="MinimumWindowHeight"/>, gets no window.
/// </summary>
public sealed record GeometryLimits
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Creates limits; every value must be positive and finite.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="minimumWindowWallLength">Shortest outdoor wall that gets a window, m.</param>
    /// <param name="minimumWindowWidth">Narrowest window placed, m.</param>
    /// <param name="minimumWindowHeight">Lowest window placed, m.</param>
    public GeometryLimits(double minimumWindowWallLength, double minimumWindowWidth, double minimumWindowHeight)
    {
        MinimumWindowWallLength = RequirePositive(minimumWindowWallLength, nameof(minimumWindowWallLength));
        MinimumWindowWidth = RequirePositive(minimumWindowWidth, nameof(minimumWindowWidth));
        MinimumWindowHeight = RequirePositive(minimumWindowHeight, nameof(minimumWindowHeight));
    }

    /// <summary>Project defaults from ADR-011.</summary>
    public static GeometryLimits Default { get; } = new(minimumWindowWallLength: 1.0, minimumWindowWidth: 0.3, minimumWindowHeight: 0.3);

    /// <summary>Shortest outdoor wall that gets a window, m.</summary>
    public double MinimumWindowWallLength { get; }

    /// <summary>Narrowest window placed, m.</summary>
    public double MinimumWindowWidth { get; }

    /// <summary>Lowest window placed, m.</summary>
    public double MinimumWindowHeight { get; }

    private static double RequirePositive(double value, string name)
    {
        if (!(value > 0) || double.IsInfinity(value))
        {
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(name, value, "Geometry limits must be positive and finite.");
        }

        return value;
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 8: Implement — replace `src/Lod.Core/Layout/LayoutMeasures.cs` (full file)**

`PlaceWindowsByRatio` takes the limits and tolerances and returns a `Result`; the window of a wall that passes the limits is the S2 window (`Window.Centered`) unchanged. `ExteriorWallArea` now names `ZoneId` through the new `using Lod.Core.Common;`.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Aggregation;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;

namespace Lod.Core.Layout;

/// <summary>Measures and window placement derived from a zone's surfaces.</summary>
public static class LayoutMeasures
{
    /// <summary>Floor area, volume, and outdoor wall area of a zone (one instance, multiplier ignored).</summary>
    /// <param name="zone">The zone.</param>
    /// <param name="surfaces">Surfaces of the layout or building containing the zone.</param>
    /// <returns>The measures.</returns>
    public static ZoneMeasures Of(Zone zone, IEnumerable<Surface> surfaces) =>
        new(zone.FloorArea, zone.Volume, ExteriorWallArea(zone.Id, surfaces));

    /// <summary>Gross area of a zone's outdoor walls, windows included.</summary>
    /// <param name="zone">Zone ID.</param>
    /// <param name="surfaces">Surfaces containing the zone's walls.</param>
    /// <returns>Area, m².</returns>
    public static double ExteriorWallArea(ZoneId zone, IEnumerable<Surface> surfaces) =>
        surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Zone == zone && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).Sum(w => w.Area);

    /// <summary>
    /// The plan generators' placement rule (D-039, D-026, ADR-011): one centered window on every outdoor wall, sized by its zone's window-to-wall
    /// ratio, except on a wall shorter than <see cref="GeometryLimits.MinimumWindowWallLength"/> or where the window would be narrower or lower
    /// than the minimum; such a wall gets no window and the info <see cref="DiagnosticCodes.WindowOmitted"/>. A value counts as below a limit
    /// only when it is smaller by more than the distance tolerance.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="surfaces">Surfaces without windows.</param>
    /// <param name="windowToWallRatio">WWR of each zone.</param>
    /// <param name="limits">Minimum wall and window geometry.</param>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    /// <returns>Surfaces with windows on outdoor walls, and an info for every omitted window.</returns>
    public static Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> PlaceWindowsByRatio(
        IEnumerable<Surface> surfaces,
        Func<ZoneId, double> windowToWallRatio,
        GeometryLimits limits,
        ToleranceSettings tolerances)
    {
        var diagnostics = new List<Diagnostic>();
        var placed = new List<Surface>();
        foreach (Surface surface in surfaces)
        {
            if (surface is not WallSurface { Boundary: BoundaryCondition.Outdoors } wall)
            {
                placed.Add(surface);
                continue;
            }

            double glazedArea = windowToWallRatio(wall.Zone) * wall.Area;
            Window? window = null;
            if (glazedArea > 0 && IsBelow(wall.Length, limits.MinimumWindowWallLength, tolerances))
            {
                diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Info(
                    DiagnosticCodes.WindowOmitted,
                    $"Outdoor wall {wall.Id.Value} is {Format(wall.Length)} m long, below the {Format(limits.MinimumWindowWallLength)} m minimum for a window (ADR-011); it has no window ({Format(glazedArea)} m² of glazing not placed).",
                    wall.Id.Value));
            }
            else if (Window.Centered(wall.Length, wall.Height, glazedArea) is { } centred)
            {
                if (IsBelow(centred.Width, limits.MinimumWindowWidth, tolerances) || IsBelow(centred.Height, limits.MinimumWindowHeight, tolerances))
                {
                    diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Info(
                        DiagnosticCodes.WindowOmitted,
                        $"The window of outdoor wall {wall.Id.Value} would be {Format(centred.Width)} m × {Format(centred.Height)} m, below the {Format(limits.MinimumWindowWidth)} m × {Format(limits.MinimumWindowHeight)} m minimum (ADR-011); the wall has no window ({Format(glazedArea)} m² of glazing not placed).",
                        wall.Id.Value));
                }
                else
                {
                    window = centred;
                }
            }

            placed.Add(wall.WithWindows(window is null ? Array.Empty<Window>() : new[] { window }));
        }

        return Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<Surface>>(placed, diagnostics);
    }

    private static bool IsBelow(double value, double limit, ToleranceSettings tolerances) => value < limit - tolerances.Distance;

    private static string Format(double value) => value.ToString("0.###", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
}
```

- [x] **Step 9: Implement — replace `src/Lod.Core/Plans/PlanGenerator.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Layout;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Core.Plans;

/// <summary>Parameters every plan generator needs. Typology-specific parameters extend this record (spec §6).</summary>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public abstract record PlanParameters(double FloorHeight, double OrientationDegrees);

/// <summary>A zone proposed by a generator's layout step.</summary>
/// <param name="Id">Zone ID.</param>
/// <param name="Name">Display name.</param>
/// <param name="SpaceType">Space type; selects the program preset.</param>
/// <param name="Footprint">Zone polygon.</param>
public sealed record PlannedZone(ZoneId Id, string Name, SpaceType SpaceType, Polygon2 Footprint);

/// <summary>The geometric output of a generator's layout step.</summary>
/// <param name="Footprint">Plan boundary.</param>
/// <param name="Zones">Zones tiling the footprint.</param>
public sealed record PlanLayout(Polygon2 Footprint, IReadOnlyList<PlannedZone> Zones);

/// <summary>
/// Base class of plan generators (spec §5). Subclasses only validate their parameters and lay out zones; this class assigns program presets,
/// builds surfaces, places centered windows from each preset's WWR within the minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011), and records provenance.
/// Generation is deterministic.
/// </summary>
/// <typeparam name="TParameters">The generator's parameter record.</typeparam>
/// <typeparam name="TPresets">The generator's preset record, one preset per space type it places (D-061).</typeparam>
public abstract class PlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets>
    where TParameters : PlanParameters
    where TPresets : PlanPresets
{
    /// <summary>Initialises the generator with the default minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011).</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    protected PlanGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : this(tolerances, GeometryLimits.Default)
    {
    }

    /// <summary>Initialises the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    /// <param name="limits">Minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011); recorded in provenance when not the default.</param>
    protected PlanGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances, GeometryLimits limits)
    {
        Tolerances = tolerances;
        Limits = limits;
    }

    /// <summary>Operation name recorded in provenance.</summary>
    public abstract string Name { get; }

    /// <summary>Tolerances.</summary>
    protected ToleranceSettings Tolerances { get; }

    /// <summary>Minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011).</summary>
    protected GeometryLimits Limits { get; }

    /// <summary>Generates a detailed plan.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Generator parameters.</param>
    /// <param name="presets">Program presets, one per slot; each must have its slot's space type.</param>
    /// <returns>The plan, or errors.</returns>
    public Result<IGeneratedPlan> Generate(TParameters parameters, TPresets presets)
    {
        var diagnostics = new List<Diagnostic>();
        if (!IsPositive(parameters.FloorHeight))
        {
            diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"FloorHeight must be positive, got {parameters.FloorHeight}."));
        }

        if (double.IsNaN(parameters.OrientationDegrees) || double.IsInfinity(parameters.OrientationDegrees))
        {
            diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"OrientationDegrees must be finite, got {parameters.OrientationDegrees}."));
        }

        diagnostics.AddRange(Validate(parameters));
        diagnostics.AddRange(CheckSlots(presets.Slots));
        if (diagnostics.Count > 0)
        {
            return Result.Failure<IGeneratedPlan>(diagnostics);
        }

        PlanLayout layout = Layout(parameters);
        var zones = new List<Zone>();
        foreach (PlannedZone planned in layout.Zones)
        {
            ProgramPreset? preset = Find(presets, planned.SpaceType);
            if (preset is null)
            {
                diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.MissingPreset, $"No program preset for space type {planned.SpaceType}.", planned.Id.Value));
                continue;
            }

            zones.Add(new Zone(planned.Id, planned.Name, planned.SpaceType, new[] { new ZonePart(planned.Footprint, 0.0, parameters.FloorHeight) }, preset.Program, Enumerable.Empty<ZoneId>()));
        }

        if (diagnostics.Count > 0)
        {
            return Result.Failure<IGeneratedPlan>(diagnostics);
        }

        Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> surfaces = new LayoutSurfaceBuilder(Tolerances)
            .Build(layout.Footprint, layout.Zones.Select(z => new LayoutZone(z.Id, z.Footprint)).ToArray(), parameters.FloorHeight);
        if (!surfaces.IsSuccess)
        {
            return Result.Failure<IGeneratedPlan>(surfaces.Diagnostics);
        }

        Dictionary<ZoneId, double> ratios = layout.Zones.ToDictionary(z => z.Id, z => Find(presets, z.SpaceType)!.WindowToWallRatio);
        Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> withWindows = LayoutMeasures.PlaceWindowsByRatio(surfaces.Value, id => ratios[id], Limits, Tolerances);
        IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> recorded = Describe(parameters)
            .Concat(new[]
            {
                new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(PlanParameters.FloorHeight), Provenance.Format(parameters.FloorHeight)),
                new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(PlanParameters.OrientationDegrees), Provenance.Format(parameters.OrientationDegrees)),
                new KeyValuePair<string, string>("Presets", string.Join(";", presets.Slots.Select(s => $"{s.Preset.SpaceType}:{s.Preset.Name}"))),
            })
            .Concat(DescribeLimits());
        var plan = new GeneratedPlan(layout.Footprint, parameters.FloorHeight, parameters.OrientationDegrees, zones, withWindows.Value, Provenance.Of(Name, recorded));
        return Result.Success<IGeneratedPlan>(plan, withWindows.Diagnostics);
    }

    /// <summary>Checks typology-specific parameters.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Errors, if any.</returns>
    protected abstract IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(TParameters parameters);

    /// <summary>Lays out zones for valid parameters.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Validated parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Footprint and zones.</returns>
    protected abstract PlanLayout Layout(TParameters parameters);

    /// <summary>Typology-specific parameters for provenance, as invariant-culture text.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Key-value pairs.</returns>
    protected abstract IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(TParameters parameters);

    private IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> DescribeLimits() => Limits == GeometryLimits.Default
        ? Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>()
        : new[]
        {
            new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(GeometryLimits.MinimumWindowWallLength), Provenance.Format(Limits.MinimumWindowWallLength)),
            new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(GeometryLimits.MinimumWindowWidth), Provenance.Format(Limits.MinimumWindowWidth)),
            new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(GeometryLimits.MinimumWindowHeight), Provenance.Format(Limits.MinimumWindowHeight)),
        };

    private static IEnumerable<Diagnostic> CheckSlots(IEnumerable<PresetSlot> slots)
    {
        foreach (PresetSlot slot in slots)
        {
            if (slot.Preset is null)
            {
                yield return Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.MissingPreset, $"Input {slot.Input} needs a {slot.SpaceType} preset, got none.", slot.Input);
            }
            else if (slot.Preset.SpaceType != slot.SpaceType)
            {
                yield return Diagnostic.Error(
                    DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch,
                    $"Input {slot.Input} needs a {slot.SpaceType} preset, got '{slot.Preset.Name}' ({slot.Preset.SpaceType}).",
                    slot.Input);
            }
        }
    }

    private static ProgramPreset? Find(TPresets presets, SpaceType spaceType) =>
        presets.Slots.FirstOrDefault(s => s.SpaceType == spaceType)?.Preset;

    /// <summary>Whether a value is finite and positive.</summary>
    /// <param name="value">The value.</param>
    /// <returns><c>true</c> when positive and finite.</returns>
    protected static bool IsPositive(double value) => value > 0 && !double.IsInfinity(value);
}
```

- [x] **Step 2: Write the shared test support — create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/PlanAssertions.cs` (full file)**

The checks every plan generator must pass (ADR-006): zones tile the footprint without overlaps, every outdoor wall carries one window centred on it with WWR × wall area of its zone's preset (D-026) and no other wall carries one, and two adjacency queries over interzone and outdoor walls.

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Generators.Tests;

/// <summary>Checks every plan generator must pass (ADR-006): tiling, the D-026 window rule, and adjacency queries.</summary>
internal static class PlanAssertions
{
    /// <summary>The zones cover the footprint's area and no two zones overlap.</summary>
    public static void TilesTheFootprint(IGeneratedPlan plan)
    {
        var ops = new PolygonOps(ToleranceSettings.Default);
        Assert.True(ToleranceSettings.Default.AreaEquals(plan.Footprint.Area, plan.Zones.Sum(z => z.FloorArea)));
        for (int i = 0; i < plan.Zones.Count; i++)
        {
            for (int j = i + 1; j < plan.Zones.Count; j++)
            {
                Assert.Equal(0.0, ops.IntersectionArea(plan.Zones[i].Parts[0].Footprint, plan.Zones[j].Parts[0].Footprint), 9);
            }
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Every outdoor wall has one window centred on it with area WWR × wall area of its zone's preset (D-026); other walls have none.</summary>
    public static void HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(IGeneratedPlan plan, PlanPresets presets)
    {
        foreach (WallSurface wall in plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>())
        {
            if (wall.Boundary != BoundaryCondition.Outdoors)
            {
                Assert.Empty(wall.Windows);
                continue;
            }

            Zone zone = plan.Zones.Single(z => z.Id == wall.Zone);
            double ratio = presets.Slots.Single(s => s.SpaceType == zone.SpaceType).Preset.WindowToWallRatio;
            Window window = Assert.Single(wall.Windows);
            Assert.Equal(ratio * wall.Area, window.Area, 9);
            Assert.Equal((wall.Length - window.Width) / 2.0, window.Offset, 12);
            Assert.Equal((wall.Height - window.Height) / 2.0, window.SillHeight, 12);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>IDs of the zones that share an interzone wall with a zone, in ordinal order.</summary>
    public static IReadOnlyList<string> Neighbours(IGeneratedPlan plan, string zone) =>
        plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>()
            .Where(w => w.Zone.Value == zone && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone)
            .Select(w => w.AdjacentZone!.Value.Value)
            .Distinct()
            .OrderBy(id => id, System.StringComparer.Ordinal)
            .ToArray();

    /// <summary>Plan orientation bins of a zone's outdoor walls, in ordinal order of their names.</summary>
    public static IReadOnlyList<OrientationBin> OutdoorFacings(IGeneratedPlan plan, string zone) =>
        plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>()
            .Where(w => w.Zone.Value == zone && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors)
            .Select(w => Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth))
            .Distinct()
            .OrderBy(b => b.ToString(), System.StringComparer.Ordinal)
            .ToArray();
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Write the failing tests — create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/StairBayBar/StairBayBarGeneratorTests.cs` (full file)**

22 tests: the ADR-006 defaults; the canonical bar (zone IDs, 51 m × 12 m, stairs 36 m², dwellings 84 m², `ST2` at x = 24–27 m, no source zones); tiling for 2, 3, and 5 bays; the window rule; each stair adjoining exactly the two dwellings of its bay and facing north and south; dwellings through the depth with a party wall between neighbouring bays and no corridor; six invalid parameters; the boundary case of one bay; every invalid parameter reported at once; preset slots; swapped presets; orientation; determinism; provenance; the canonical snapshot.

```csharp
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Generators.Tests.StairBayBar;

public sealed class StairBayBarGeneratorTests
{
    /// <summary>The example presets, one per slot.</summary>
    public static readonly StairBayBarPresets Examples = new(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair);

    private readonly StairBayBarGenerator _generator = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void DefaultsAreTheAdr006Defaults()
    {
        Assert.Equal(new StairBayBarParameters(BayCount: 3, UnitWidth: 7.0, Depth: 12.0, StairWidth: 3.0, FloorHeight: 3.0, OrientationDegrees: 0.0), StairBayBarParameters.Default);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalBarHasThreeStairBays()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(StairBayBarParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "ST1", "ST2", "ST3", "U1", "U2", "U3", "U4", "U5", "U6" }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal(51.0 * 12.0, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.Stair), z => Assert.Equal(36.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.DwellingUnit), z => Assert.Equal(84.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Stair 2", "Unit 4" }, plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value is "ST2" or "U4").Select(z => z.Name));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(24.0, 0.0), new Point2(27.0, 0.0), new Point2(27.0, 12.0), new Point2(24.0, 12.0) }, plan.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "ST2").Parts[0].Footprint.Outer);
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Empty(z.SourceZones));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2)]
    [InlineData(3)]
    [InlineData(5)]
    public void ZonesTileTheFootprint(int bays)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(StairBayBarParameters.Default with { BayCount = bays });

        PlanAssertions.TilesTheFootprint(plan);
        Assert.Equal(3 * bays, plan.Zones.Count);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryOutdoorWallHasOneCentredWindowSizedByItsPreset()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(StairBayBarParameters.Default, Examples);

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        PlanAssertions.HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, Examples);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EachStairServesExactlyTheTwoDwellingsOfItsBay()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(StairBayBarParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "U1", "U2" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "ST1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "U3", "U4" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "ST2"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "U5", "U6" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "ST3"));
        Assert.All(new[] { "ST1", "ST2", "ST3" }, st => Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.South }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, st)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void DwellingsRunThroughTheDepthAndNeighbouringBaysShareAPartyWall()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(StairBayBarParameters.Default);

        Assert.DoesNotContain(plan.Zones, z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.Corridor);
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.DwellingUnit), z => Assert.Contains(OrientationBin.North, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, z.Id.Value)));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.DwellingUnit), z => Assert.Contains(OrientationBin.South, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, z.Id.Value)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "ST1", "U3" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "U2"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "ST2", "U2" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "U3"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.South, OrientationBin.West }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "U1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.South }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "U6"));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(1, 7.0, 12.0, 3.0, "BayCount")]
    [InlineData(0, 7.0, 12.0, 3.0, "BayCount")]
    [InlineData(3, 0.0, 12.0, 3.0, "UnitWidth")]
    [InlineData(3, 7.0, -1.0, 3.0, "Depth")]
    [InlineData(3, 7.0, 12.0, double.NaN, "StairWidth")]
    [InlineData(3, 7.0, double.PositiveInfinity, 3.0, "Depth")]
    public void InvalidParametersAreErrors(int bays, double unitWidth, double depth, double stairWidth, string name)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new StairBayBarParameters(bays, unitWidth, depth, stairWidth, 3.0, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.StartsWith(name, error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ASingleBayIsTheStairPairFamily()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(StairBayBarParameters.Default with { BayCount = 1 }, Examples);

        Assert.Equal("BayCount must be at least 2 (one bay is HDHM-TYP-008, Stair Pair), got 1.", Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics).Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryInvalidParameterIsReported()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new StairBayBarParameters(0, -7.0, 0.0, -3.0, 0.0, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.Equal(5, result.Diagnostics.Count(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PresetsHaveOneSlotPerSpaceTypeInOrder()
    {
        Assert.Equal(new[] { ("DwellingUnit", SpaceType.DwellingUnit), ("Stair", SpaceType.Stair) }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => (s.Input, s.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { Examples.DwellingUnit, Examples.Stair }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => s.Preset));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SwappedPresetsAreAnErrorPerWrongSlot()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(StairBayBarParameters.Default, new StairBayBarPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair, ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit));

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "DwellingUnit", "Stair" }, result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch).Select(d => d.Subject));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OrientationRotatesFacadeBins()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(StairBayBarParameters.Default with { OrientationDegrees = 90.0 });
        WallSurface south = plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().First(w => w.Zone.Value == "U1" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth) == OrientationBin.South);

        Assert.Equal(OrientationBin.West, Orientation.Bin(south.PlanAzimuth + plan.OrientationDegrees));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GenerationIsDeterministic()
    {
        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(Generate(StairBayBarParameters.Default)), TextReport.Describe(Generate(StairBayBarParameters.Default)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsParametersAndPresets()
    {
        Provenance provenance = Generate(StairBayBarParameters.Default).Provenance;

        Assert.Equal(nameof(StairBayBarGenerator), provenance.Operation);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "BayCount=3", "Depth=12", "FloorHeight=3", "OrientationDegrees=0", "Presets=DwellingUnit:Example Dwelling Unit;Stair:Example Stair", "StairWidth=3", "UnitWidth=7" },
            provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanSnapshot()
    {
        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(Generate(StairBayBarParameters.Default)), "stair-bay-bar-canonical");
    }

    private IGeneratedPlan Generate(StairBayBarParameters parameters) => _generator.Generate(parameters, Examples).Value;
}
```

- [x] **Step 5: Implement — create `src/Lod.Generators/StairBayBar/StairBayLayout.cs` (full file)**

The last bay ends exactly at `bays · bayWidth`, so the footprint and the zones share their east edge without rounding.

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;

/// <summary>
/// The stair bay of ADR-006: dwelling | stair | dwelling side by side along a bar, each through the full depth, the stair serving the two dwellings
/// beside it. Shared by <see cref="StairBayBarGenerator"/> (HDHM-TYP-001) and the stair pair (HDHM-TYP-008), so the bay geometry exists once.
/// </summary>
internal static class StairBayLayout
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Lays out <paramref name="bays"/> stair bays west to east from the origin. Zones: <c>ST1…STn</c> (stairs, west to east), then <c>U1…U2n</c>
    /// (dwellings, west to east); bay <c>i</c> is <c>U(2i−1)</c>, <c>STi</c>, <c>U(2i)</c>.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="bays">Number of bays, at least 1.</param>
    /// <param name="unitWidth">Dwelling width along the bar, m.</param>
    /// <param name="depth">Bar depth, m.</param>
    /// <param name="stairWidth">Stair width along the bar, m.</param>
    /// <returns>The footprint and the zones.</returns>
    public static PlanLayout Bays(int bays, double unitWidth, double depth, double stairWidth)
    {
        double bayWidth = (2.0 * unitWidth) + stairWidth;
        var stairs = new List<PlannedZone>();
        var units = new List<PlannedZone>();
        for (int i = 0; i < bays; i++)
        {
            double west = i * bayWidth;
            double stairWest = west + unitWidth;
            double stairEast = stairWest + stairWidth;
            double east = i == bays - 1 ? bays * bayWidth : west + bayWidth;
            stairs.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"ST{i + 1}"), $"Stair {i + 1}", SpaceType.Stair, Polygon2.Rectangle(stairWest, 0.0, stairEast, depth)));
            units.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"U{(2 * i) + 1}"), $"Unit {(2 * i) + 1}", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Polygon2.Rectangle(west, 0.0, stairWest, depth)));
            units.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"U{(2 * i) + 2}"), $"Unit {(2 * i) + 2}", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Polygon2.Rectangle(stairEast, 0.0, east, depth)));
        }

        stairs.AddRange(units);
        return new PlanLayout(Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, bays * bayWidth, depth), stairs);
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 6: Implement — create `src/Lod.Generators/StairBayBar/StairBayBarGenerator.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;

/// <summary>Parameters of the stair-bay bar (HDHM-TYP-001, ADR-006).</summary>
/// <param name="BayCount">Number of stair bays along the bar; at least 2.</param>
/// <param name="UnitWidth">Width of each dwelling along the bar, m.</param>
/// <param name="Depth">Bar depth, which every dwelling and stair spans, m.</param>
/// <param name="StairWidth">Width of each stair along the bar, m.</param>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public sealed record StairBayBarParameters(
    int BayCount,
    double UnitWidth,
    double Depth,
    double StairWidth,
    double FloorHeight,
    double OrientationDegrees)
    : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, OrientationDegrees)
{
    /// <summary>The ADR-006 defaults: 3 bays of 7 m dwellings and a 3 m stair, 12 m deep, 3 m storeys, orientation 0°.</summary>
    public static StairBayBarParameters Default { get; } = new(3, 7.0, 12.0, 3.0, 3.0, 0.0);
}

/// <summary>The program presets of the stair-bay bar, one per space type it places (D-061).</summary>
/// <param name="DwellingUnit">Preset of the dwellings; space type <see cref="SpaceType.DwellingUnit"/>.</param>
/// <param name="Stair">Preset of the stairs; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Stair"/>.</param>
public sealed record StairBayBarPresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit, ProgramPreset Stair) : PlanPresets
{
    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
    {
        new PresetSlot(nameof(DwellingUnit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, DwellingUnit),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Stair), SpaceType.Stair, Stair),
    };
}

/// <summary>
/// Repeated stair bays in a linear bar (HDHM-TYP-001, ADR-006): <see cref="StairBayBarParameters.BayCount"/> bays of dwelling | stair | dwelling,
/// each through the full depth. Each stair serves the two dwellings of its bay on every storey (R002); there is no corridor or gallery, stairs never
/// adjoin each other, and neighbouring bays meet dwelling to dwelling (R003); one straight tract (R001). Fewer than two bays is rejected (R004, R005):
/// one bay is HDHM-TYP-008. One zone per dwelling unit (D-009).
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>Zone IDs: <c>ST1…STn</c> stairs west to east, then <c>U1…U2n</c> dwellings west to east; bay <c>i</c> is <c>U(2i−1)</c>, <c>STi</c>, <c>U(2i)</c>.</remarks>
public sealed class StairBayBarGenerator : PlanGenerator<StairBayBarParameters, StairBayBarPresets>
{
    /// <summary>Creates the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public StairBayBarGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(StairBayBarGenerator);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(StairBayBarParameters parameters)
    {
        if (parameters.BayCount < 2)
        {
            yield return Diagnostic.Error(
                DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter,
                $"{nameof(StairBayBarParameters.BayCount)} must be at least 2 (one bay is HDHM-TYP-008, Stair Pair), got {parameters.BayCount}.");
        }

        var values = new (string Name, double Value)[]
        {
            (nameof(StairBayBarParameters.UnitWidth), parameters.UnitWidth),
            (nameof(StairBayBarParameters.Depth), parameters.Depth),
            (nameof(StairBayBarParameters.StairWidth), parameters.StairWidth),
        };
        foreach ((string name, double value) in values.Where(v => !IsPositive(v.Value)))
        {
            yield return Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"{name} must be positive, got {value}.");
        }
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override PlanLayout Layout(StairBayBarParameters p) => StairBayLayout.Bays(p.BayCount, p.UnitWidth, p.Depth, p.StairWidth);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(StairBayBarParameters p) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.BayCount), p.BayCount.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.UnitWidth), Provenance.Format(p.UnitWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.Depth), Provenance.Format(p.Depth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.StairWidth), Provenance.Format(p.StairWidth)),
    };
}
```

### Task 6: Stair pair generator (`HDHM-TYP-008`)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/Lod.Generators/StairPair/StairPairGenerator.cs`
- Test: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/StairPair/StairPairGeneratorTests.cs`
- Snapshot: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/StairPair/Snapshots/stair-pair-canonical.txt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `StairBayLayout.Bays` (Task 5; internal to `Lod.Generators`), `StairBayBarGenerator` and its records (in the test that compares the pair with one bay of the bar), `PlanAssertions` (Task 5), the `PlanGenerator` base.
- Produces: `public sealed record StairPairParameters(double UnitWidth, double Depth, double StairWidth, double FloorHeight, double OrientationDegrees) : PlanParameters` with `static Default` = (7, 12, 3, 3, 0); `public sealed record StairPairPresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit, ProgramPreset Stair) : PlanPresets`; `public sealed class StairPairGenerator(ToleranceSettings)`, `Name` = `StairPairGenerator`, layout `StairBayLayout.Bays(1, …)` (zones `ST1`, `U1`, `U2`); `InvalidParameter` for each length that is not positive and finite; provenance keys `UnitWidth`, `Depth`, `StairWidth`.

- [x] **Step 1: Write the failing tests — create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/StairPair/StairPairGeneratorTests.cs` (full file)**

12 tests: defaults; the canonical plate (`ST1`, `U1`, `U2`, 17 m × 12 m, tiling); the stair adjoining both dwellings, which adjoin only the stair; the pair equal, zone by zone, to the first bay of a two-bay stair-bay bar with the same widths; the window rule; three invalid lengths; preset slots; determinism; provenance; the canonical snapshot.

```csharp
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;
using Lod.Generators.StairPair;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Generators.Tests.StairPair;

public sealed class StairPairGeneratorTests
{
    /// <summary>The example presets, one per slot.</summary>
    public static readonly StairPairPresets Examples = new(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair);

    private readonly StairPairGenerator _generator = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void DefaultsAreTheAdr006Defaults()
    {
        Assert.Equal(new StairPairParameters(UnitWidth: 7.0, Depth: 12.0, StairWidth: 3.0, FloorHeight: 3.0, OrientationDegrees: 0.0), StairPairParameters.Default);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanIsOneDwellingPairAroundOneStair()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(StairPairParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "ST1", "U1", "U2" }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal(17.0 * 12.0, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 36.0, 84.0, 84.0 }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.FloorArea));
        PlanAssertions.TilesTheFootprint(plan);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheStairServesBothDwellingsOnOppositeSides()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(StairPairParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "U1", "U2" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "ST1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "ST1" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "U1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "ST1" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "U2"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.South, OrientationBin.West }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "U1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.South }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "U2"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ThePairIsOneBayOfTheStairBayBar()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan pair = Generate(new StairPairParameters(6.5, 11.0, 3.5, 3.0, 0.0));
        IGeneratedPlan bar = new StairBayBarGenerator(ToleranceSettings.Default)
            .Generate(new StairBayBarParameters(2, 6.5, 11.0, 3.5, 3.0, 0.0), new StairBayBarPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair)).Value;

        foreach (string id in new[] { "ST1", "U1", "U2" })
        {
            Assert.Equal(bar.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == id).Parts[0].Footprint.Outer, pair.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == id).Parts[0].Footprint.Outer);
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryOutdoorWallHasOneCentredWindowSizedByItsPreset()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(StairPairParameters.Default, Examples);

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        PlanAssertions.HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, Examples);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0.0, 12.0, 3.0, "UnitWidth")]
    [InlineData(7.0, -12.0, 3.0, "Depth")]
    [InlineData(7.0, 12.0, double.NegativeInfinity, "StairWidth")]
    public void InvalidParametersAreErrors(double unitWidth, double depth, double stairWidth, string name)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new StairPairParameters(unitWidth, depth, stairWidth, 3.0, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.StartsWith(name, error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PresetsHaveOneSlotPerSpaceTypeInOrder()
    {
        Assert.Equal(new[] { ("DwellingUnit", SpaceType.DwellingUnit), ("Stair", SpaceType.Stair) }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => (s.Input, s.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { Examples.DwellingUnit, Examples.Stair }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => s.Preset));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GenerationIsDeterministic()
    {
        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(Generate(StairPairParameters.Default)), TextReport.Describe(Generate(StairPairParameters.Default)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsParametersAndPresets()
    {
        Provenance provenance = Generate(StairPairParameters.Default).Provenance;

        Assert.Equal(nameof(StairPairGenerator), provenance.Operation);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "Depth=12", "FloorHeight=3", "OrientationDegrees=0", "Presets=DwellingUnit:Example Dwelling Unit;Stair:Example Stair", "StairWidth=3", "UnitWidth=7" },
            provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanSnapshot()
    {
        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(Generate(StairPairParameters.Default)), "stair-pair-canonical");
    }

    private IGeneratedPlan Generate(StairPairParameters parameters) => _generator.Generate(parameters, Examples).Value;
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Implement — create `src/Lod.Generators/StairPair/StairPairGenerator.cs` (full file)**

The layout is the stair bay of Task 5, not a copy of it.

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;

namespace Lod.Generators.StairPair;

/// <summary>Parameters of the stair pair (HDHM-TYP-008, ADR-006).</summary>
/// <param name="UnitWidth">Width of each dwelling, m.</param>
/// <param name="Depth">Plate depth, which both dwellings and the stair span, m.</param>
/// <param name="StairWidth">Width of the stair between the dwellings, m.</param>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public sealed record StairPairParameters(
    double UnitWidth,
    double Depth,
    double StairWidth,
    double FloorHeight,
    double OrientationDegrees)
    : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, OrientationDegrees)
{
    /// <summary>The ADR-006 defaults: two 7 m dwellings around a 3 m stair, 12 m deep, 3 m storeys, orientation 0°.</summary>
    public static StairPairParameters Default { get; } = new(7.0, 12.0, 3.0, 3.0, 0.0);
}

/// <summary>The program presets of the stair pair, one per space type it places (D-061).</summary>
/// <param name="DwellingUnit">Preset of the two dwellings; space type <see cref="SpaceType.DwellingUnit"/>.</param>
/// <param name="Stair">Preset of the stair; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Stair"/>.</param>
public sealed record StairPairPresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit, ProgramPreset Stair) : PlanPresets
{
    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
    {
        new PresetSlot(nameof(DwellingUnit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, DwellingUnit),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Stair), SpaceType.Stair, Stair),
    };
}

/// <summary>
/// One stair between a dwelling pair (HDHM-TYP-008, ADR-006): one stair bay of the stair-bay bar as its own building, laid out by the same code.
/// One compact linear plate with one dwelling pair (R001); the dwellings lie on opposite sides of the stair (R002), which adjoins and serves both
/// (R003). One zone per dwelling unit (D-009).
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>Zone IDs: <c>ST1</c> stair, <c>U1</c> west dwelling, <c>U2</c> east dwelling.</remarks>
public sealed class StairPairGenerator : PlanGenerator<StairPairParameters, StairPairPresets>
{
    /// <summary>Creates the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public StairPairGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(StairPairGenerator);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(StairPairParameters parameters)
    {
        var values = new (string Name, double Value)[]
        {
            (nameof(StairPairParameters.UnitWidth), parameters.UnitWidth),
            (nameof(StairPairParameters.Depth), parameters.Depth),
            (nameof(StairPairParameters.StairWidth), parameters.StairWidth),
        };
        return values
            .Where(v => !IsPositive(v.Value))
            .Select(v => Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"{v.Name} must be positive, got {v.Value}."))
            .ToArray();
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override PlanLayout Layout(StairPairParameters p) => StairBayLayout.Bays(1, p.UnitWidth, p.Depth, p.StairWidth);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(StairPairParameters p) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.UnitWidth), Provenance.Format(p.UnitWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.Depth), Provenance.Format(p.Depth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.StairWidth), Provenance.Format(p.StairWidth)),
    };
}
```

- [x] **Step 1: Write the failing tests — create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/GalleryBar/GalleryBarGeneratorTests.cs` (full file)**

18 tests: defaults; the canonical bar (`ST1`, `ST2`, `U1`–`U6`, 44 m × 10 m, stairs 40 m², dwellings 60 m²); tiling for 2, 6, and 9 dwellings; the window rule; no gallery or corridor zone, the footprint exactly the 44 m × 10 m bar, and both long façades of every dwelling outdoors; the end stairs adjoining the end dwellings; five invalid parameters; one module; preset slots; determinism; provenance; the canonical snapshot.

```csharp
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.GalleryBar;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Generators.Tests.GalleryBar;

public sealed class GalleryBarGeneratorTests
{
    /// <summary>The example presets, one per slot.</summary>
    public static readonly GalleryBarPresets Examples = new(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair);

    private readonly GalleryBarGenerator _generator = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void DefaultsAreTheAdr006Defaults()
    {
        Assert.Equal(new GalleryBarParameters(UnitCount: 6, UnitWidth: 6.0, Depth: 10.0, StairWidth: 4.0, FloorHeight: 3.0, OrientationDegrees: 0.0), GalleryBarParameters.Default);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalBarHasARowOfDwellingsBetweenTwoEndStairs()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(GalleryBarParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "ST1", "ST2", "U1", "U2", "U3", "U4", "U5", "U6" }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal(44.0 * 10.0, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.Stair), z => Assert.Equal(40.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.DwellingUnit), z => Assert.Equal(60.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(40.0, 0.0), new Point2(44.0, 0.0), new Point2(44.0, 10.0), new Point2(40.0, 10.0) }, plan.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "ST2").Parts[0].Footprint.Outer);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2)]
    [InlineData(6)]
    [InlineData(9)]
    public void ZonesTileTheFootprint(int units)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(GalleryBarParameters.Default with { UnitCount = units });

        PlanAssertions.TilesTheFootprint(plan);
        Assert.Equal(units + 2, plan.Zones.Count);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryOutdoorWallHasOneCentredWindowSizedByItsPreset()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(GalleryBarParameters.Default, Examples);

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        PlanAssertions.HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, Examples);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheGalleryIsNotModelledAndBothLongFacadesAreOutdoors()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(GalleryBarParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { SpaceType.Stair, SpaceType.DwellingUnit }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType).Distinct());
        Assert.Equal(Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, 44.0, 10.0).Outer, plan.Footprint.Outer);
        Assert.All(
            plan.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.DwellingUnit),
            z => Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.South }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, z.Id.Value)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheEndStairsAdjoinTheEndDwellings()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(GalleryBarParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "U1" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "ST1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "U6" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "ST2"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.South, OrientationBin.West }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "ST1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.South }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "ST2"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "U2", "U4" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "U3"));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(1, 6.0, 10.0, 4.0, "UnitCount")]
    [InlineData(-2, 6.0, 10.0, 4.0, "UnitCount")]
    [InlineData(6, -6.0, 10.0, 4.0, "UnitWidth")]
    [InlineData(6, 6.0, 0.0, 4.0, "Depth")]
    [InlineData(6, 6.0, 10.0, double.NaN, "StairWidth")]
    public void InvalidParametersAreErrors(int units, double unitWidth, double depth, double stairWidth, string name)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new GalleryBarParameters(units, unitWidth, depth, stairWidth, 3.0, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.StartsWith(name, error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OneModuleIsNotAGalleryBar()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(GalleryBarParameters.Default with { UnitCount = 1 }, Examples);

        Assert.Equal("UnitCount must be at least 2 (a gallery serves successive dwellings), got 1.", Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics).Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PresetsHaveOneSlotPerSpaceTypeInOrder()
    {
        Assert.Equal(new[] { ("DwellingUnit", SpaceType.DwellingUnit), ("Stair", SpaceType.Stair) }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => (s.Input, s.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { Examples.DwellingUnit, Examples.Stair }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => s.Preset));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GenerationIsDeterministic()
    {
        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(Generate(GalleryBarParameters.Default)), TextReport.Describe(Generate(GalleryBarParameters.Default)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsParametersAndPresets()
    {
        Provenance provenance = Generate(GalleryBarParameters.Default).Provenance;

        Assert.Equal(nameof(GalleryBarGenerator), provenance.Operation);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "Depth=10", "FloorHeight=3", "OrientationDegrees=0", "Presets=DwellingUnit:Example Dwelling Unit;Stair:Example Stair", "StairWidth=4", "UnitCount=6", "UnitWidth=6" },
            provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanSnapshot()
    {
        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(Generate(GalleryBarParameters.Default)), "gallery-bar-canonical");
    }

    private IGeneratedPlan Generate(GalleryBarParameters parameters) => _generator.Generate(parameters, Examples).Value;
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Implement — create `src/Lod.Generators/GalleryBar/GalleryBarGenerator.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Generators.GalleryBar;

/// <summary>Parameters of the gallery bar (HDHM-TYP-002, ADR-006).</summary>
/// <param name="UnitCount">Number of dwellings along the bar; at least 2.</param>
/// <param name="UnitWidth">Width of each dwelling along the bar, m.</param>
/// <param name="Depth">Bar depth, which every dwelling and both stairs span, m.</param>
/// <param name="StairWidth">Width of each end stair along the bar, m.</param>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public sealed record GalleryBarParameters(
    int UnitCount,
    double UnitWidth,
    double Depth,
    double StairWidth,
    double FloorHeight,
    double OrientationDegrees)
    : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, OrientationDegrees)
{
    /// <summary>The ADR-006 defaults: 6 dwellings of 6 m between two 4 m end stairs, 10 m deep, 3 m storeys, orientation 0°.</summary>
    public static GalleryBarParameters Default { get; } = new(6, 6.0, 10.0, 4.0, 3.0, 0.0);
}

/// <summary>The program presets of the gallery bar, one per space type it places (D-061).</summary>
/// <param name="DwellingUnit">Preset of the dwellings; space type <see cref="SpaceType.DwellingUnit"/>.</param>
/// <param name="Stair">Preset of the two end stairs; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Stair"/>.</param>
public sealed record GalleryBarPresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit, ProgramPreset Stair) : PlanPresets
{
    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
    {
        new PresetSlot(nameof(DwellingUnit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, DwellingUnit),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Stair), SpaceType.Stair, Stair),
    };
}

/// <summary>
/// Continuous external gallery beside a linear bar (HDHM-TYP-002, ADR-006): one straight row of dwellings through the bar depth (R001) with a
/// full-depth stair at each end, from which the gallery would run past every dwelling (R002, R003). The gallery itself is not modelled (D-083): no
/// gallery zone and no gallery geometry; the façade on the gallery side is an ordinary outdoor façade. Fewer than two dwellings is rejected (R004,
/// R005). One zone per dwelling unit (D-009).
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>Zone IDs: <c>ST1</c> west stair, <c>ST2</c> east stair, then <c>U1…Un</c> dwellings west to east.</remarks>
public sealed class GalleryBarGenerator : PlanGenerator<GalleryBarParameters, GalleryBarPresets>
{
    /// <summary>Creates the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public GalleryBarGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(GalleryBarGenerator);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(GalleryBarParameters parameters)
    {
        if (parameters.UnitCount < 2)
        {
            yield return Diagnostic.Error(
                DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter,
                $"{nameof(GalleryBarParameters.UnitCount)} must be at least 2 (a gallery serves successive dwellings), got {parameters.UnitCount}.");
        }

        var values = new (string Name, double Value)[]
        {
            (nameof(GalleryBarParameters.UnitWidth), parameters.UnitWidth),
            (nameof(GalleryBarParameters.Depth), parameters.Depth),
            (nameof(GalleryBarParameters.StairWidth), parameters.StairWidth),
        };
        foreach ((string name, double value) in values.Where(v => !IsPositive(v.Value)))
        {
            yield return Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"{name} must be positive, got {value}.");
        }
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override PlanLayout Layout(GalleryBarParameters p)
    {
        double eastStair = p.StairWidth + (p.UnitCount * p.UnitWidth);
        double length = eastStair + p.StairWidth;
        var zones = new List<PlannedZone>
        {
            new(new ZoneId("ST1"), "Stair 1", SpaceType.Stair, Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, p.StairWidth, p.Depth)),
            new(new ZoneId("ST2"), "Stair 2", SpaceType.Stair, Polygon2.Rectangle(eastStair, 0.0, length, p.Depth)),
        };
        for (int i = 0; i < p.UnitCount; i++)
        {
            double west = p.StairWidth + (i * p.UnitWidth);
            double east = i == p.UnitCount - 1 ? eastStair : west + p.UnitWidth;
            zones.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"U{i + 1}"), $"Unit {i + 1}", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Polygon2.Rectangle(west, 0.0, east, p.Depth)));
        }

        return new PlanLayout(Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, length, p.Depth), zones);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(GalleryBarParameters p) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.UnitCount), p.UnitCount.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.UnitWidth), Provenance.Format(p.UnitWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.Depth), Provenance.Format(p.Depth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.StairWidth), Provenance.Format(p.StairWidth)),
    };
}
```

- [x] **Step 1: Write the failing tests — create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/TerraceRow/TerraceRowGeneratorTests.cs` (full file)**

17 tests: defaults; the canonical row (`U1`–`U6` named *House 1*–*House 6*, 33 m × 10 m, 55 m² each, dwelling units only); tiling for 2, 6, and 11 plots; the window rule; neighbouring plots sharing a dividing wall; plots 0.8 m wide, whose 0.8 m fronts get no window and twelve `WindowOmitted` infos while the two 10 m end walls keep theirs; four invalid parameters; one house; the single preset slot; determinism; provenance; the canonical snapshot.

```csharp
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.TerraceRow;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Generators.Tests.TerraceRow;

public sealed class TerraceRowGeneratorTests
{
    /// <summary>The example preset of the only slot.</summary>
    public static readonly TerraceRowPresets Examples = new(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit);

    private readonly TerraceRowGenerator _generator = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void DefaultsAreTheAdr006Defaults()
    {
        Assert.Equal(new TerraceRowParameters(PlotCount: 6, PlotWidth: 5.5, Depth: 10.0, FloorHeight: 3.0, OrientationDegrees: 0.0), TerraceRowParameters.Default);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalRowHasOneDwellingPerPlot()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(TerraceRowParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "U1", "U2", "U3", "U4", "U5", "U6" }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "House 1", "House 6" }, plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value is "U1" or "U6").Select(z => z.Name));
        Assert.Equal(33.0 * 10.0, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Equal(55.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Equal(SpaceType.DwellingUnit, z.SpaceType));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2)]
    [InlineData(6)]
    [InlineData(11)]
    public void ZonesTileTheFootprint(int plots)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(TerraceRowParameters.Default with { PlotCount = plots });

        PlanAssertions.TilesTheFootprint(plan);
        Assert.Equal(plots, plan.Zones.Count);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryOutdoorWallHasOneCentredWindowSizedByItsPreset()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(TerraceRowParameters.Default, Examples);

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        PlanAssertions.HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, Examples);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NeighbouringPlotsShareADividingWall()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(TerraceRowParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "U2" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "U1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "U2", "U4" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "U3"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "U5" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "U6"));
        WallSurface party = plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Single(w => w.Zone.Value == "U3" && w.AdjacentZone?.Value == "U4");
        Assert.Equal(10.0, party.Length, 9);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.South, OrientationBin.West }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "U1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.South }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "U3"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void FrontsBelowTheMinimumWallLengthGetNoWindow()
    {
        // 0.8 m plots: every front and back wall is below ADR-011's 1 m minimum; the 10 m end walls keep their windows.
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(TerraceRowParameters.Default with { PlotWidth = 0.8 }, Examples);

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess);
        WallSurface[] outdoor = result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).ToArray();
        Assert.All(outdoor.Where(w => w.Length < 1.0), w => Assert.Empty(w.Windows));
        Assert.Equal(2, outdoor.Count(w => w.Windows.Count == 1));
        Assert.Equal(12, result.Diagnostics.Count(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.WindowOmitted && d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Info));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(1, 5.5, 10.0, "PlotCount")]
    [InlineData(0, 5.5, 10.0, "PlotCount")]
    [InlineData(6, 0.0, 10.0, "PlotWidth")]
    [InlineData(6, 5.5, double.NaN, "Depth")]
    public void InvalidParametersAreErrors(int plots, double plotWidth, double depth, string name)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new TerraceRowParameters(plots, plotWidth, depth, 3.0, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.StartsWith(name, error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OneHouseIsNotATerrace()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(TerraceRowParameters.Default with { PlotCount = 1 }, Examples);

        Assert.Equal("PlotCount must be at least 2 (attached plots share a dividing wall), got 1.", Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics).Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PresetsHaveOneDwellingUnitSlot()
    {
        PresetSlotAssert(Examples);
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(TerraceRowParameters.Default, new TerraceRowPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "DwellingUnit" }, result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch).Select(d => d.Subject));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GenerationIsDeterministic()
    {
        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(Generate(TerraceRowParameters.Default)), TextReport.Describe(Generate(TerraceRowParameters.Default)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsParametersAndPresets()
    {
        Provenance provenance = Generate(TerraceRowParameters.Default).Provenance;

        Assert.Equal(nameof(TerraceRowGenerator), provenance.Operation);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "Depth=10", "FloorHeight=3", "OrientationDegrees=0", "PlotCount=6", "PlotWidth=5.5", "Presets=DwellingUnit:Example Dwelling Unit" },
            provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanSnapshot()
    {
        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(Generate(TerraceRowParameters.Default)), "terrace-row-canonical");
    }

    private static void PresetSlotAssert(TerraceRowPresets presets)
    {
        Assert.Equal(new[] { ("DwellingUnit", SpaceType.DwellingUnit) }, presets.Slots.Select(s => (s.Input, s.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Same(presets.DwellingUnit, Assert.Single(presets.Slots).Preset);
    }

    private IGeneratedPlan Generate(TerraceRowParameters parameters) => _generator.Generate(parameters, Examples).Value;
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Implement — create `src/Lod.Generators/TerraceRow/TerraceRowGenerator.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Generators.TerraceRow;

/// <summary>Parameters of the terrace row (HDHM-TYP-006, ADR-006).</summary>
/// <param name="PlotCount">Number of attached house plots along the row; at least 2.</param>
/// <param name="PlotWidth">Width of each plot along the row, m.</param>
/// <param name="Depth">Row depth, which every house spans, m.</param>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public sealed record TerraceRowParameters(
    int PlotCount,
    double PlotWidth,
    double Depth,
    double FloorHeight,
    double OrientationDegrees)
    : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, OrientationDegrees)
{
    /// <summary>The ADR-006 defaults: 6 plots of 5.5 m, 10 m deep, 3 m storeys, orientation 0°.</summary>
    public static TerraceRowParameters Default { get; } = new(6, 5.5, 10.0, 3.0, 0.0);
}

/// <summary>The program preset of the terrace row, which places only dwellings (D-061).</summary>
/// <param name="DwellingUnit">Preset of the houses; space type <see cref="SpaceType.DwellingUnit"/>.</param>
public sealed record TerraceRowPresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit) : PlanPresets
{
    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
    {
        new PresetSlot(nameof(DwellingUnit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, DwellingUnit),
    };
}

/// <summary>
/// Attached narrow terrace row (HDHM-TYP-006, ADR-006): one straight row (R001) of repeated attached house plots (R002), neighbouring plots
/// sharing a dividing wall (R003); fewer than two plots is rejected (R004, R005). Each house is one dwelling zone per storey; its own stair is inside
/// the dwelling and not zoned (D-009), so the row has no stair or corridor zones. A house of several storeys is one zone per storey, stacked by the
/// floor aggregators.
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>Zone IDs: <c>U1…Un</c> houses west to east, named <c>House i</c>.</remarks>
public sealed class TerraceRowGenerator : PlanGenerator<TerraceRowParameters, TerraceRowPresets>
{
    /// <summary>Creates the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public TerraceRowGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(TerraceRowGenerator);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(TerraceRowParameters parameters)
    {
        if (parameters.PlotCount < 2)
        {
            yield return Diagnostic.Error(
                DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter,
                $"{nameof(TerraceRowParameters.PlotCount)} must be at least 2 (attached plots share a dividing wall), got {parameters.PlotCount}.");
        }

        var values = new (string Name, double Value)[]
        {
            (nameof(TerraceRowParameters.PlotWidth), parameters.PlotWidth),
            (nameof(TerraceRowParameters.Depth), parameters.Depth),
        };
        foreach ((string name, double value) in values.Where(v => !IsPositive(v.Value)))
        {
            yield return Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"{name} must be positive, got {value}.");
        }
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override PlanLayout Layout(TerraceRowParameters p)
    {
        double length = p.PlotCount * p.PlotWidth;
        var zones = new List<PlannedZone>();
        for (int i = 0; i < p.PlotCount; i++)
        {
            double west = i * p.PlotWidth;
            double east = i == p.PlotCount - 1 ? length : west + p.PlotWidth;
            zones.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"U{i + 1}"), $"House {i + 1}", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Polygon2.Rectangle(west, 0.0, east, p.Depth)));
        }

        return new PlanLayout(Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, length, p.Depth), zones);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(TerraceRowParameters p) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.PlotCount), p.PlotCount.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.PlotWidth), Provenance.Format(p.PlotWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.Depth), Provenance.Format(p.Depth)),
    };
}
```

```csharp
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.Linear;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Generators.Tests.Linear;

public sealed class LinearPlanGeneratorTests
{
    /// <summary>24 m × 18 m, 4 m stair, two 10 m units per row.</summary>
    public static readonly LinearPlanParameters Canonical = new(
        Length: 24.0,
        UnitDepth: 8.0,
        CorridorWidth: 2.0,
        TargetUnitWidth: 10.0,
        StairLength: 4.0,
        FloorHeight: 3.0,
        OrientationDegrees: 0.0);

    /// <summary>The example presets, one per slot.</summary>
    public static readonly LinearPlanPresets Examples = new(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair);

    private readonly LinearPlanGenerator _generator = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(10.0, 2)]
    [InlineData(8.0, 3)]
    [InlineData(6.0, 3)]
    [InlineData(100.0, 1)]
    public void UnitsPerRowRoundsHalfAwayFromZero(double targetWidth, int expected)
    {
        Assert.Equal(expected, LinearPlanGenerator.UnitsPerRow(Canonical with { TargetUnitWidth = targetWidth }));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanHasStairCorridorAndTwoUnitsPerRow()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(Canonical);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "ST", "CO", "US1", "US2", "UN1", "UN2" }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal(24.0 * 18.0, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal(80.0, plan.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "US1").FloorArea, 9);
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Empty(z.SourceZones));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ZonesTileTheFootprintWithoutOverlap()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(Canonical with { TargetUnitWidth = 6.0 });
        var ops = new PolygonOps(ToleranceSettings.Default);

        Assert.True(ToleranceSettings.Default.AreaEquals(plan.Footprint.Area, plan.Zones.Sum(z => z.FloorArea)));
        foreach (Zone a in plan.Zones)
        {
            foreach (Zone b in plan.Zones.Where(b => b.Id.Value.CompareTo(a.Id.Value) > 0))
            {
                Assert.Equal(0.0, ops.IntersectionArea(a.Parts[0].Footprint, b.Parts[0].Footprint), 9);
            }
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryOutdoorWallHasOneCenteredWindowSizedByItsPreset()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(Canonical);

        foreach (WallSurface wall in plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors))
        {
            Zone zone = plan.Zones.Single(z => z.Id == wall.Zone);
            double ratio = Examples.Slots.Single(s => s.SpaceType == zone.SpaceType).Preset.WindowToWallRatio;
            Window window = Assert.Single(wall.Windows);
            Assert.Equal(ratio * wall.Area, window.Area, 9);
            Assert.Equal((wall.Length - window.Width) / 2.0, window.Offset, 12);
            Assert.Equal((wall.Height - window.Height) / 2.0, window.SillHeight, 12);
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void InterzoneWallsComeInMatchingPairs()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(Canonical with { TargetUnitWidth = 6.0 });
        WallSurface[] interzone = plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone).ToArray();

        Assert.NotEmpty(interzone);
        Assert.All(interzone, w => Assert.Contains(interzone, o => o.Zone == w.AdjacentZone && o.AdjacentZone == w.Zone && o.Start == w.End && o.End == w.Start));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StairHasThreeOutdoorWallsAndThreeNeighbours()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(Canonical);
        WallSurface[] stair = plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Zone.Value == "ST").ToArray();

        Assert.Equal(3, stair.Count(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "CO", "UN1", "US1" }, stair.Where(w => w.AdjacentZone.HasValue).Select(w => w.AdjacentZone!.Value.Value).OrderBy(x => x));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(10.0)]
    [InlineData(6.0)]
    [InlineData(100.0)]
    public void RealisesTheDoubleSidedCorridorBarFamily(double targetWidth)
    {
        // HDHM-TYP-009 (ADR-006): R001 and R003, dwellings on both flanks of the internal strip, each adjoining it; R002, the shared route runs
        // inside the bar from the stair to the far façade.
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(Canonical with { TargetUnitWidth = targetWidth });
        string[] south = plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.StartsWith("US")).Select(z => z.Id.Value).ToArray();
        string[] north = plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.StartsWith("UN")).Select(z => z.Id.Value).ToArray();

        Assert.NotEmpty(south);
        Assert.Equal(south.Length, north.Length);
        Assert.All(south.Concat(north), id => Assert.Contains("CO", PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, id)));
        Assert.Contains("ST", PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "CO"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "CO"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { SpaceType.Stair, SpaceType.Corridor, SpaceType.DwellingUnit }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType).Distinct());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OrientationRotatesFacadeBins()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(Canonical with { OrientationDegrees = 90.0 });
        WallSurface south = plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Single(w => w.Zone.Value == "US1" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);

        Assert.Equal(OrientationBin.West, Orientation.Bin(south.PlanAzimuth + plan.OrientationDegrees));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0.0, 8.0, 2.0, 10.0, 4.0)]
    [InlineData(24.0, -1.0, 2.0, 10.0, 4.0)]
    [InlineData(24.0, 8.0, 2.0, 10.0, 24.0)]
    public void InvalidParametersAreErrors(double length, double unitDepth, double corridor, double unitWidth, double stair)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new LinearPlanParameters(length, unitDepth, corridor, unitWidth, stair, 3.0, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.Contains(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PresetsHaveOneSlotPerSpaceTypeInOrder()
    {
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { ("DwellingUnit", SpaceType.DwellingUnit), ("Corridor", SpaceType.Corridor), ("Stair", SpaceType.Stair) },
            Examples.Slots.Select(s => (s.Input, s.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { Examples.DwellingUnit, Examples.Corridor, Examples.Stair }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => s.Preset));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SwappedPresetsAreAnErrorPerWrongSlot()
    {
        var swapped = new LinearPlanPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor, ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair);

        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(Canonical, swapped);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "DwellingUnit", "Corridor" },
            result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch).Select(d => d.Subject));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void MissingPresetIsAnError()
    {
        var withoutStair = new LinearPlanPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor, null!);

        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(Canonical, withoutStair);

        Assert.Contains(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.MissingPreset && d.Subject == "Stair");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GenerationIsDeterministic()
    {
        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(Generate(Canonical)), TextReport.Describe(Generate(Canonical)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsParametersAndPresets()
    {
        Provenance provenance = Generate(Canonical).Provenance;

        Assert.Equal(nameof(LinearPlanGenerator), provenance.Operation);
        Assert.Contains(provenance.Parameters, p => p.Key == "TargetUnitWidth" && p.Value == "10");
        Assert.Contains(provenance.Parameters, p => p.Key == "Presets" && p.Value.Contains("DwellingUnit:Example Dwelling Unit"));
        Assert.Equal(BuildInfo.InformationalVersion, provenance.CodeVersion);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanSnapshot()
    {
        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(Generate(Canonical)), "linear-plan-canonical");
    }

    private IGeneratedPlan Generate(LinearPlanParameters parameters) => _generator.Generate(parameters, Examples).Value;
}
```
