```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Generators.Linear;

/// <summary>Parameters of the linear block plan.</summary>
/// <param name="Length">Plan length along X, m.</param>
/// <param name="UnitDepth">Depth of each dwelling unit row, m.</param>
/// <param name="CorridorWidth">Corridor width, m.</param>
/// <param name="TargetUnitWidth">Desired unit width; the actual width divides the row evenly, m.</param>
/// <param name="StairLength">Length of the stair at the west end, m.</param>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public sealed record LinearPlanParameters(
    double Length,
    double UnitDepth,
    double CorridorWidth,
    double TargetUnitWidth,
    double StairLength,
    double FloorHeight,
    double OrientationDegrees)
    : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, OrientationDegrees);

/// <summary>The program presets of the linear block, one per space type it places (D-061).</summary>
/// <param name="DwellingUnit">Preset of the dwelling units; space type <see cref="SpaceType.DwellingUnit"/>.</param>
/// <param name="Corridor">Preset of the corridor; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Corridor"/>.</param>
/// <param name="Stair">Preset of the stair; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Stair"/>.</param>
public sealed record LinearPlanPresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit, ProgramPreset Corridor, ProgramPreset Stair) : PlanPresets
{
    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
    {
        new PresetSlot(nameof(DwellingUnit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, DwellingUnit),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Corridor), SpaceType.Corridor, Corridor),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Stair), SpaceType.Stair, Stair),
    };
}

/// <summary>
/// The simplest linear block (S2): a rectangle with a full-depth stair at the west end and a double-loaded corridor running east to the
/// façade, with one row of equal dwelling units on each side. One zone per dwelling unit (D-009). This is the generator of the double-sided internal
/// corridor bar, HDHM-TYP-009 (ADR-006): the unit rows flank the corridor strip (R001, R003) and the corridor runs inside the bar from the stair to
/// the east façade (R002).
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>
/// Zone IDs: <c>ST</c> stair, <c>CO</c> corridor, <c>US1..USn</c> south units west to east, <c>UN1..UNn</c> north units west to east.
/// </remarks>
public sealed class LinearPlanGenerator : PlanGenerator<LinearPlanParameters, LinearPlanPresets>
{
    /// <summary>Creates the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public LinearPlanGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(LinearPlanGenerator);

    /// <summary>Number of units per row: the row length divided by the target width, rounded half away from zero, at least 1.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Units per row.</returns>
    public static int UnitsPerRow(LinearPlanParameters parameters) =>
        Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Round((parameters.Length - parameters.StairLength) / parameters.TargetUnitWidth, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero));

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(LinearPlanParameters parameters)
    {
        var values = new (string Name, double Value)[]
        {
            (nameof(LinearPlanParameters.Length), parameters.Length),
            (nameof(LinearPlanParameters.UnitDepth), parameters.UnitDepth),
            (nameof(LinearPlanParameters.CorridorWidth), parameters.CorridorWidth),
            (nameof(LinearPlanParameters.TargetUnitWidth), parameters.TargetUnitWidth),
            (nameof(LinearPlanParameters.StairLength), parameters.StairLength),
        };
        foreach ((string name, double value) in values.Where(v => !IsPositive(v.Value)))
        {
            yield return Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"{name} must be positive, got {value}.");
        }

        if (parameters.Length <= parameters.StairLength)
        {
            yield return Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"Length ({parameters.Length}) must exceed StairLength ({parameters.StairLength}).");
        }
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override PlanLayout Layout(LinearPlanParameters p)
    {
        int count = UnitsPerRow(p);
        double unitWidth = (p.Length - p.StairLength) / count;
        double[] xs = Enumerable.Range(0, count + 1).Select(i => i == count ? p.Length : p.StairLength + (i * unitWidth)).ToArray();
        double corridorSouth = p.UnitDepth;
        double corridorNorth = p.UnitDepth + p.CorridorWidth;
        double depth = corridorNorth + p.UnitDepth;

        var zones = new List<PlannedZone>
        {
            new(new ZoneId("ST"), "Stair", SpaceType.Stair, Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, p.StairLength, depth)),
            new(new ZoneId("CO"), "Corridor", SpaceType.Corridor, Polygon2.Rectangle(p.StairLength, corridorSouth, p.Length, corridorNorth)),
        };
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
        {
            zones.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"US{i + 1}"), $"Unit S{i + 1}", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Polygon2.Rectangle(xs[i], 0.0, xs[i + 1], corridorSouth)));
        }

        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
        {
            zones.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"UN{i + 1}"), $"Unit N{i + 1}", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Polygon2.Rectangle(xs[i], corridorNorth, xs[i + 1], depth)));
        }

        return new PlanLayout(Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, p.Length, depth), zones);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(LinearPlanParameters p) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.Length), Provenance.Format(p.Length)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.UnitDepth), Provenance.Format(p.UnitDepth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.CorridorWidth), Provenance.Format(p.CorridorWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.TargetUnitWidth), Provenance.Format(p.TargetUnitWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.StairLength), Provenance.Format(p.StairLength)),
    };
}
```

### Task 10: Every bar family with every simplifier and aggregator

**Files:**
- Test: create `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/BarTypologyTests.cs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the four generators and their `Default` records (Tasks 5–8); test support `Pipeline.Simplifier(string name)`, `Pipeline.Aggregator(string name)`, `Pipeline.Tolerances` (S4.2); `PlanTransform.Apply(IGeneratedPlan plan, double rotationDegrees, double translationX, double translationY) : Result<IGeneratedPlan>` (S4.2); `SemanticMerge`, `FloorEntry(IFloor, int)`, `BuildingValidator(ToleranceSettings).Validate(IGeneratedBuilding) : ValidationReport`.
- Produces: the S8 test of the roadmap: each of the four families × four simplifiers × four aggregators (floors 1, 3, 1) builds and validates without warnings, on the canonical plan and on the plan rotated by 131.4° and moved by (15.08, −153.9) m; *Semantic Merge* joins only connected zones of one space type for each family. No production code changes.

Like Task 9, this task has no red phase: it checks that the existing simplifiers and aggregators accept the new families unchanged (D-084: "Bars need no pipeline change"). If any case fails, stop and report: a pipeline change needs its own decision and test-first task.

- [x] **Step 1: Write the tests — create `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/BarTypologyTests.cs` (full file)**

132 cases: `EveryCombinationValidates` (64), `EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan` (64), and `SemanticMergeJoinsOnlyConnectedZonesOfOneType` (4: the three stairs of the stair-bay bar stay apart while the dwellings of neighbouring bays merge in pairs; the stair pair keeps three zones; the gallery bar's two end stairs stay apart; the terrace row becomes one zone).

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Lod.Generators.GalleryBar;
using Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;
using Lod.Generators.StairPair;
using Lod.Generators.TerraceRow;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>The S8.1 bar families (ADR-006) through every plan simplifier and floor aggregator.</summary>
public sealed class BarTypologyTests
{
    private static readonly string[] Families = { "StairBayBar", "StairPair", "GalleryBar", "TerraceRow" };
    private static readonly string[] Simplifiers = { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" };
    private static readonly string[] Aggregators = { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from family in Families
        from simplifier in Simplifiers
        from aggregator in Aggregators
        select new object[] { family, simplifier, aggregator };

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidates(string family, string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        AssertValidates(Plan(family), simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan(string family, string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        AssertValidates(PlanTransform.Apply(Plan(family), 131.4, 15.08, -153.9).Value, simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("StairBayBar", "DwellingUnit-1,DwellingUnit-2,DwellingUnit-3,DwellingUnit-4,Stair-1,Stair-2,Stair-3")]
    [InlineData("StairPair", "DwellingUnit-1,DwellingUnit-2,Stair-1")]
    [InlineData("GalleryBar", "DwellingUnit-1,Stair-1,Stair-2")]
    [InlineData("TerraceRow", "DwellingUnit-1")]
    public void SemanticMergeJoinsOnlyConnectedZonesOfOneType(string family, string zones)
    {
        // Stairs of a stair-bay bar are separated by dwellings and stay apart; the dwellings of two neighbouring bays share a party wall and merge.
        IFloor floor = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(Plan(family)).Value;

        Assert.Equal(zones, string.Join(",", floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value).OrderBy(id => id, StringComparer.Ordinal)));
    }

    private static void AssertValidates(IGeneratedPlan plan, string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        Result<IFloor> floor = Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(plan);
        Assert.True(floor.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", floor.Diagnostics));
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(floor.Value, 1), new(floor.Value, 3), new(floor.Value, 1) };

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(entries);
        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Warning);
    }

    /// <summary>The canonical plan of a bar family: ADR-006 defaults and the example presets.</summary>
    private static IGeneratedPlan Plan(string family)
    {
        ProgramPreset unit = ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit;
        ProgramPreset stair = ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair;
        return family switch
        {
            "StairBayBar" => new StairBayBarGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(StairBayBarParameters.Default, new StairBayBarPresets(unit, stair)).Value,
            "StairPair" => new StairPairGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(StairPairParameters.Default, new StairPairPresets(unit, stair)).Value,
            "GalleryBar" => new GalleryBarGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(GalleryBarParameters.Default, new GalleryBarPresets(unit, stair)).Value,
            "TerraceRow" => new TerraceRowGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(TerraceRowParameters.Default, new TerraceRowPresets(unit)).Value,
            _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(family), family, "Unknown family."),
        };
    }
}
```

using System;
using System.Drawing;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.GalleryBar;
using Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;
using Lod.Generators.StairPair;
using Lod.Generators.TerraceRow;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Parameters;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Components;

/// <summary>
/// Shared inputs and output of the typology plan generator components (ADR-006): number inputs with the family's defaults, one required preset input
/// per space type (D-061, D-065), the Preview Location last (D-062), and one Plan output.
/// </summary>
public abstract class TypologyGeneratorComponent : GH_Component
{
    protected const string FloorHeightDescription = "Floor-to-floor height, m.";

    protected const string OrientationDescription = "Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.";

    protected const string Metres = " All lengths in metres regardless of model units.";

    protected TypologyGeneratorComponent(string name, string nickname, string description)
        : base(name, nickname, description + Metres, ComponentCategories.Category, ComponentCategories.Generate)
    {
    }

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new PlanParameter(), "Plan", "Plan", "The detailed plan.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    /// <summary>Adds the required preset input of one space type.</summary>
    protected static void AddPreset(GH_InputParamManager pManager, string name, string nickname, SpaceType spaceType, string role) =>
        pManager.AddParameter(new ProgramPresetParameter(), name, nickname, $"Program preset of {role}; space type {spaceType}.", GH_ParamAccess.item);

    /// <summary>Reads <paramref name="count"/> number inputs from <paramref name="first"/>; <c>false</c> when one is missing.</summary>
    protected static bool TryNumbers(IGH_DataAccess DA, int first, int count, out double[] values)
    {
        values = new double[count];
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
        {
            if (!DA.GetData(first + i, ref values[i]))
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    /// <summary>Reads a preset input; <c>null</c> when it is unconnected, which Grasshopper reports as a missing input (D-065).</summary>
    protected static ProgramPreset? Preset(IGH_DataAccess DA, int index)
    {
        ProgramPresetGoo? goo = null;
        return DA.GetData(index, ref goo) && goo is not null ? goo.Value : null;
    }

    /// <summary>Reports the generator's diagnostics and outputs the plan with this component's preview location.</summary>
    protected void Output(IGH_DataAccess DA, Result<IGeneratedPlan> result)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan? plan = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, result);
        if (plan is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new PlanGoo(plan, ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA)));
        }
    }
}

public sealed class StairBayBarComponent : TypologyGeneratorComponent
{
    public StairBayBarComponent()
        : base(
            "Stair Bay Bar",
            "SBar",
            "Repeated stair bays in a linear bar (HDHM-TYP-001, ADR-006): bays of dwelling | stair | dwelling through the full depth, each stair serving the two dwellings beside it; no corridor. One zone per dwelling unit.")
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("911c3204-42bb-4023-bdb7-2931ea1caad3");

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        StairBayBarParameters d = StairBayBarParameters.Default;
        pManager.AddIntegerParameter("Bay Count", "BC", "Number of stair bays along the bar; at least 2.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.BayCount);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Unit Width", "UW", "Width of each dwelling along the bar, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.UnitWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Depth", "D", "Bar depth, which every dwelling and stair spans, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.Depth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Stair Width", "SW", "Width of each stair along the bar, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.StairWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Floor Height", "FH", FloorHeightDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.FloorHeight);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Orientation", "O", OrientationDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.OrientationDegrees);
        AddPreset(pManager, "Dwelling Unit", "DU", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, "the dwellings");
        AddPreset(pManager, "Stair", "St", SpaceType.Stair, "the stairs");
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        int bays = 0;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref bays) || !TryNumbers(DA, 1, 5, out double[] v) || Preset(DA, 6) is not { } dwellingUnit || Preset(DA, 7) is not { } stair)
        {
            return;
        }

        var parameters = new StairBayBarParameters(bays, v[0], v[1], v[2], v[3], v[4]);
        Output(DA, new StairBayBarGenerator(ToleranceSettings.Default).Generate(parameters, new StairBayBarPresets(dwellingUnit, stair)));
    }
}

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Grasshopper.Kernel.Types;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Generators.Linear;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Parameters;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Components;

public sealed class LinearPlanGeneratorComponent : GH_Component
{
    public LinearPlanGeneratorComponent()
        : base(
            "Linear Plan Generator",
            "Linear",
            "Detailed linear block plan, the double-sided internal corridor bar (HDHM-TYP-009, ADR-006): west stair, double-loaded corridor, one zone per dwelling unit. All lengths in metres regardless of model units.",
            ComponentCategories.Category,
            ComponentCategories.Generate)
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("a4a9131e-0a0f-49e0-99a4-f26760f6810f");

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Length", "L", "Plan length along X, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 24.0);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Unit Depth", "UD", "Depth of each unit row, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 8.0);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Corridor Width", "CW", "Corridor width, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 2.0);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Unit Width", "UW", "Target unit width, m; the row is divided evenly.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 10.0);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Stair Length", "SL", "Length of the west stair, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 4.0);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Floor Height", "FH", "Floor-to-floor height, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 3.0);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Orientation", "O", "Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.", GH_ParamAccess.item, 0.0);
        pManager.AddParameter(new ProgramPresetParameter(), "Dwelling Unit", "DU", "Program preset of the dwelling units; space type DwellingUnit.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
        pManager.AddParameter(new ProgramPresetParameter(), "Corridor", "Co", "Program preset of the corridor; space type Corridor.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
        pManager.AddParameter(new ProgramPresetParameter(), "Stair", "St", "Program preset of the stair; space type Stair.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new PlanParameter(), "Plan", "Plan", "The detailed plan.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        double[] values = new double[7];
        for (int i = 0; i < values.Length; i++)
        {
            if (!DA.GetData(i, ref values[i]))
            {
                return;
            }
        }

        ProgramPresetGoo? dwellingUnit = null;
        ProgramPresetGoo? corridor = null;
        ProgramPresetGoo? stair = null;
        if (!DA.GetData(7, ref dwellingUnit) || !DA.GetData(8, ref corridor) || !DA.GetData(9, ref stair)
            || dwellingUnit is null || corridor is null || stair is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        var parameters = new LinearPlanParameters(values[0], values[1], values[2], values[3], values[4], values[5], values[6]);
        var presets = new LinearPlanPresets(dwellingUnit.Value, corridor.Value, stair.Value);
        IGeneratedPlan? plan = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, new LinearPlanGenerator(ToleranceSettings.Default).Generate(parameters, presets));
        if (plan is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new PlanGoo(plan, ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA)));
        }
    }
}

public sealed class NoSimplificationComponent : GH_Component
{
    public NoSimplificationComponent()
        : base("No Simplification", "Z0", "Keeps the detailed plan's zones and surfaces (Z0).", ComponentCategories.Category, ComponentCategories.Simplify)
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("83270abe-a13f-4f4c-a893-79739ad6e006");

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new PlanParameter(), "Plan", "Plan", "Detailed plan.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new FloorParameter(), "Floor", "Floor", "The floor.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        PlanGoo? plan = null;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref plan) || plan is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        IFloor? floor = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, new NoSimplification().Simplify(plan.Value));
        if (floor is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new FloorGoo(floor, ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA)));
        }
    }
}

/// <summary>Shared inputs and solving of the floor aggregator components: floors bottom to top and their multipliers.</summary>
public abstract class FloorAggregatorComponent : GH_Component
{
    protected FloorAggregatorComponent(string name, string nickname, string description)
        : base(name, nickname, description, ComponentCategories.Category, ComponentCategories.Aggregate)
    {
    }

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    protected abstract string MultiplierDescription { get; }

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new FloorParameter(), "Floors", "F", "Floors, bottom to top.", GH_ParamAccess.list);
        pManager.AddIntegerParameter("Multipliers", "N", MultiplierDescription, GH_ParamAccess.list);
        pManager[1].Optional = true;
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new BuildingParameter(), "Building", "Bldg", "The building.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        var floors = new List<FloorGoo>();
        var multipliers = new List<int>();
        if (!DA.GetDataList(0, floors) || floors.Any(f => f is null))
        {
            return;
        }

        DA.GetDataList(1, multipliers);
        if (multipliers.Count != 0 && multipliers.Count != floors.Count)
        {
            AddRuntimeMessage(GH_RuntimeMessageLevel.Error, $"Give one multiplier per floor ({floors.Count}), or none for all 1.");
            return;
        }

        FloorEntry[] entries = floors.Select((f, i) => new FloorEntry(f.Value, multipliers.Count == 0 ? 1 : multipliers[i])).ToArray();
        IGeneratedBuilding? building = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, CreateAggregator().Aggregate(entries));
        if (building is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new BuildingGoo(building, ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA), UnmodelledParts(entries)));
        }
    }

    protected abstract IFloorAggregator CreateAggregator();

    /// <summary>Parts of the storeys the building represents but does not model, for its preview; none unless the method leaves storeys out.</summary>
    protected virtual IReadOnlyList<ZonePart> UnmodelledParts(IReadOnlyList<FloorEntry> entries) => new ZonePart[0];
}

public sealed class StackFloorsComponent : FloorAggregatorComponent
{
    public StackFloorsComponent()
        : base("Stack Floors", "Stack", "All storeys explicit: each floor is repeated by its multiplier and stacked; inter-storey surfaces are interzone.")
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("6ce6524d-09b0-49fc-9b88-4c15bdcc8797");

    protected override string MultiplierDescription => "How many storeys each floor stands for (repeated explicitly). Default 1.";

    protected override IFloorAggregator CreateAggregator() => new Stack(ToleranceSettings.Default);
}

public sealed class InspectComponent : GH_Component
{
    public InspectComponent()
        : base("Inspect", "Inspect", "Text report and provenance of a plan, floor, or building.", ComponentCategories.Category, ComponentCategories.Inspect)
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("1d56a553-7a66-451f-90e2-81e744b43bdd");

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddGenericParameter("Object", "O", "A plan, floor, or building.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddTextParameter("Report", "R", "Zones, loads, and surfaces.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
        pManager.AddTextParameter("Provenance", "P", "How the object was produced, with code version.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        IGH_Goo? goo = null;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref goo) || goo is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        (string Report, Provenance Provenance)? described = goo switch
        {
            PlanGoo plan => (TextReport.Describe(plan.Value), plan.Value.Provenance),
            FloorGoo floor => (TextReport.Describe(floor.Value), floor.Value.Provenance),
            BuildingGoo building => (TextReport.Describe(building.Value), building.Value.Provenance),
            _ => null,
        };
        if (described is null)
        {
            AddRuntimeMessage(GH_RuntimeMessageLevel.Error, $"Expected a plan, floor, or building, got {goo.TypeName}.");
            return;
        }

        DA.SetData(0, described.Value.Report);
        DA.SetData(1, described.Value.Provenance.Describe());
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 2: Replace `scripts/make_icons.py` (full file)**

The four motifs are added after `linear_generator`, and the four icon entries after *Linear Plan Generator* in the Generate group; every other motif and entry is unchanged.

```python
"""Draws the BEMGen Grasshopper icons (D-063).

Every icon is drawn at 96 x 96 pixels on a transparent background and downsampled to 24 x 24 with LANCZOS, then written to
src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons/<Name>.png, where <Name> is the class name of the component, parameter, or assembly info that shows it.
Each icon is a dark glyph plus the accent colour of its toolbar panel; a parameter uses the panel of the component that makes its object.
Shapes only, no fonts, so the output does not depend on installed fonts.

Run from the repository root:  python scripts/make_icons.py
Requires Python 3.10+ and Pillow. Generated with Pillow 12.3.0; the same Pillow version gives the same pixels.
The script stops with an error when an icon has no class in src/Lod.Grasshopper or a class has no icon.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import sys
from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
SOURCE = ROOT / "src" / "Lod.Grasshopper"
OUTPUT = SOURCE / "Icons"

CANVAS = 96
SIZE = 24
LINE = 8  # 2 px after downsampling

DARK = (38, 38, 38, 255)
WHITE = (255, 255, 255, 255)

# One accent per toolbar panel (ComponentCategories).
INFO = (96, 125, 139, 255)
PROGRAM = (230, 140, 30, 255)
GENERATE = (46, 158, 79, 255)
SIMPLIFY = (47, 123, 216, 255)
AGGREGATE = (138, 79, 200, 255)
CONVERT = (208, 69, 58, 255)
INSPECT = (24, 150, 150, 255)

Colour = tuple[int, int, int, int]
Draw = ImageDraw.ImageDraw
Motif = Callable[[Draw, Colour], None]
Painter = Callable[[Image.Image, Colour], None]


def box(d: Draw, xy: tuple[float, float, float, float], fill=None, outline=DARK, width: int = LINE) -> None:
    d.rectangle(xy, fill=fill, outline=outline, width=width)


def line(d: Draw, points: list[tuple[float, float]], width: int = LINE, fill=DARK) -> None:
    d.line(points, fill=fill, width=width, joint="curve")


def arrow_head(d: Draw, tip: tuple[float, float], direction: str, size: float, fill=DARK) -> None:
    x, y = tip
    if direction == "right":
        d.polygon([(x, y), (x - size, y - size), (x - size, y + size)], fill=fill)
    elif direction == "down":
        d.polygon([(x, y), (x - size, y - size), (x + size, y - size)], fill=fill)


# --- motifs: each draws inside the 96 x 96 canvas -------------------------------------------------------------


def info(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((6, 6, 90, 90), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.rectangle((42, 40, 54, 74), fill=WHITE)
    d.rectangle((42, 20, 54, 32), fill=WHITE)


def assembly(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A stepped block: the building at three levels of detail.
    box(d, (8, 52, 88, 88), fill=accent)
    box(d, (8, 30, 60, 56), fill=accent)
    box(d, (8, 8, 36, 34), fill=accent)


def schedule(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A 24-hour profile as bars on a time axis.
    heights = [16, 48, 72, 36, 60, 76, 24]
    for i, h in enumerate(heights):
        x = 12 + i * 12
        d.rectangle((x, 84 - h, x + 7, 83), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((4, 84, 92, 91), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((4, 4, 11, 91), fill=DARK)


def load(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    bolt = [(58, 4), (18, 54), (44, 54), (34, 92), (78, 38), (52, 38), (64, 4)]
    d.polygon(bolt, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)


def preset_tag(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    tag = [(8, 30), (30, 8), (88, 8), (88, 88), (30, 88), (8, 66)]
    d.polygon(tag, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((24, 40, 40, 56), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
    line(d, [(52, 34), (76, 34)], width=6)
    line(d, [(52, 48), (76, 48)], width=6)
    line(d, [(52, 62), (76, 62)], width=6)


def house(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(48, 6), (90, 44), (6, 44)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    box(d, (16, 42, 80, 90), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((40, 60, 56, 90), fill=DARK)


def corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (4, 30, 92, 66), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(16, 48), (60, 48)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (78, 48), "right", 12)


def stair(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    steps = [(6, 90), (6, 70), (28, 70), (28, 50), (50, 50), (50, 30), (72, 30), (72, 10), (90, 10), (90, 90)]
    d.polygon(steps, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)


def linear_generator(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Footprint with a west stair, a corridor band, and dwelling-unit divisions on both sides.
    box(d, (4, 12, 91, 83))
    d.rectangle((12, 20, 23, 75), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((28, 40, 87, 55), fill=accent)
    for x in (48, 68):
        line(d, [(x, 12), (x, 40)])
        line(d, [(x, 55), (x, 83)])


def stair_bay_bar(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A long bar of two stair bays: dwelling (accent) | stair (dark) | dwelling, repeated, with a party wall between the bays.
    box(d, (2, 30, 93, 66), fill=accent)
    for x in (20, 66):
        d.rectangle((x, 30, x + 10, 66), fill=DARK)
    line(d, [(48, 30), (48, 66)], width=4)


def stair_pair(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # One compact plate: two dwellings (accent) around one full-depth stair (dark).
    box(d, (14, 22, 82, 74), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((42, 26, 54, 70), fill=DARK)


def gallery_bar(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A bar of dwellings (accent) between two end stairs (dark); the gallery along it is dashed, as it is not modelled.
    box(d, (4, 34, 91, 76), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((8, 38, 18, 72), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((77, 38, 87, 72), fill=DARK)
    for x in (36, 54):
        line(d, [(x, 34), (x, 76)], width=6)
    for x in range(6, 90, 16):
        line(d, [(x, 20), (x + 9, 20)], width=6)


def terrace_row(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Attached houses in elevation: three gables sharing their side walls.
    houses = [(6, 34), (34, 62), (62, 90)]
    for left, right in houses:
        middle = (left + right) / 2
        d.polygon([(left, 46), (middle, 22), (right, 46), (right, 88), (left, 88)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    for left, right in houses:
        d.rectangle((left + 10, 68, right - 10, 88), fill=DARK)


def transform_plan(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A footprint outline and the same footprint rotated and moved, joined by a turning arrow.
    box(d, (6, 52, 50, 90), width=6)
    d.polygon([(66, 12), (92, 28), (76, 56), (50, 40)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    d.arc((18, 20, 70, 72), start=180, end=270, fill=DARK, width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (58, 24), "right", 10)


def z0(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Many cells: every unit its own zone.
    box(d, (4, 4, 91, 91), fill=accent)
    for k in (32, 60):
        line(d, [(k, 4), (k, 91)])
        line(d, [(4, k), (91, k)])


def z1(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Cells of one space type merged: two unit rows and a corridor.
    box(d, (4, 10, 92, 40), fill=accent)
    box(d, (4, 56, 92, 86), fill=accent)
    box(d, (4, 36, 92, 60))


def z2(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Perimeter ring split on the corner bisectors, plus a core.
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92))
    d.rectangle((30, 30, 66, 66), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    for a, b in [((4, 4), (30, 30)), ((92, 4), (66, 30)), ((4, 92), (30, 66)), ((92, 92), (66, 66))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=6)


def z3(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (6, 16, 90, 80), fill=accent)


def slab(d: Draw, accent, y: float, height: float = 16, left: float = 6, right: float = 90) -> None:
    box(d, (left, y, right, y + height), fill=accent, width=6)


def stack(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    for y in (8, 36, 64):
        slab(d, accent, y, 24)


def multiplier(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    slab(d, accent, 6, 24, 6, 90)
    line(d, [(18, 44), (50, 84)], width=10)
    line(d, [(50, 44), (18, 84)], width=10)
    for y in (44, 58, 72):
        d.rectangle((62, y, 90, y + 8), fill=DARK)


def single_zone_per_floor_type(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # One zone per floor entry: a one-storey zone below a three-storey zone.
    box(d, (24, 66, 71, 91), fill=accent)
    box(d, (24, 4, 71, 58), fill=accent)


def single_zone_building(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (24, 4, 71, 91), fill=accent)


def convert(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(40, 44), (90, 44), (90, 92), (40, 92)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    line(d, [(6, 14), (44, 14), (64, 34), (64, 56)], width=10)
    arrow_head(d, (64, 74), "down", 16)


def inspect(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((6, 6, 64, 64), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((18, 18, 38, 38), fill=WHITE)
    line(d, [(56, 56), (88, 88)], width=16)


def mini_grid(d: Draw, origin: tuple[float, float], size: float, cells: int, accent) -> None:
    x0, y0 = origin
    box(d, (x0, y0, x0 + size, y0 + size), fill=accent, width=6)
    step = size / cells
    for k in range(1, cells):
        line(d, [(x0 + k * step, y0), (x0 + k * step, y0 + size)], width=4)
        line(d, [(x0, y0 + k * step), (x0 + size, y0 + k * step)], width=4)


def map_source_target(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    mini_grid(d, (4, 4), 40, 2, accent)
    box(d, (52, 52, 92, 92), fill=accent, width=6)
    line(d, [(32, 56), (32, 72), (40, 72)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (52, 72), "right", 12)
    line(d, [(56, 26), (72, 26), (72, 36)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (72, 50), "down", 12)


def validate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    line(d, [(24, 50), (42, 68), (74, 30)], width=14, fill=WHITE)


# Parameter motifs: small versions of their object's motif, drawn inside the hexagon (x and y in 20..76).


def small_schedule(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    for x, h in ((28, 16), (40, 36), (52, 24), (64, 40)):
        d.rectangle((x, 72 - h, x + 7, 71), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((24, 72, 75, 75), fill=DARK)


def small_load(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(54, 20), (30, 52), (46, 52), (40, 76), (68, 44), (52, 44), (60, 20)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=4)


def small_preset(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(20, 36), (32, 24), (76, 24), (76, 72), (32, 72), (20, 60)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=4)
    d.ellipse((30, 42, 42, 54), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)


def small_plan(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (20, 24, 75, 71), width=4)
    d.rectangle((24, 44, 71, 51), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((24, 28, 31, 67), fill=DARK)
    for x in (48,):
        line(d, [(x, 24), (x, 44)], width=4)
        line(d, [(x, 52), (x, 71)], width=4)


def small_floor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (16, 36, 79, 59), fill=accent, width=4)


def small_building(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (32, 16, 63, 79), fill=accent, width=4)
    for y in (36, 56):
        line(d, [(32, y), (63, y)], width=4)


# --- icons ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def component(motif: Motif) -> Painter:
    """A component: its motif at full size."""

    def paint(image: Image.Image, accent: Colour) -> None:
        motif(ImageDraw.Draw(image), accent)

    return paint


def parameter(motif: Motif) -> Painter:
    """A parameter: a small version of its object's motif inside a hexagon (the Grasshopper parameter shape)."""

    def paint(image: Image.Image, accent: Colour) -> None:
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(image)
        d.polygon([(24, 4), (72, 4), (94, 48), (72, 92), (24, 92), (2, 48)], fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
        motif(d, accent)

    return paint


ICONS: dict[str, tuple[Colour, Painter]] = {
    "BemGenAssemblyInfo": (INFO, component(assembly)),
    "BemGenInfoComponent": (INFO, component(info)),
    "ScheduleComponent": (PROGRAM, component(schedule)),
    "LoadComponent": (PROGRAM, component(load)),
    "ProgramPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(preset_tag)),
    "DwellingUnitPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(house)),
    "CorridorPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(corridor)),
    "StairPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(stair)),
    "LinearPlanGeneratorComponent": (GENERATE, component(linear_generator)),
    "StairBayBarComponent": (GENERATE, component(stair_bay_bar)),
    "StairPairComponent": (GENERATE, component(stair_pair)),
    "GalleryBarComponent": (GENERATE, component(gallery_bar)),
    "TerraceRowComponent": (GENERATE, component(terrace_row)),
    "TransformPlanComponent": (GENERATE, component(transform_plan)),
    "NoSimplificationComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z0)),
    "SemanticMergeComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z1)),
    "PerimeterCoreComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z2)),
    "SingleZonePerFloorComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z3)),
    "StackFloorsComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(stack)),
    "StackedFloorZoneMultiplierComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(multiplier)),
    "SingleZonePerFloorTypeComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(single_zone_per_floor_type)),
    "SingleZoneBuildingComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(single_zone_building)),
    "Convert2BemComponent": (CONVERT, component(convert)),
    "InspectComponent": (INSPECT, component(inspect)),
    "MapSourceToTargetComponent": (INSPECT, component(map_source_target)),
    "ValidateComponent": (INSPECT, component(validate)),
    "ScheduleParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_schedule)),
    "LoadParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_load)),
    "ProgramPresetParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_preset)),
    "PlanParameter": (GENERATE, parameter(small_plan)),
    "FloorParameter": (SIMPLIFY, parameter(small_floor)),
    "BuildingParameter": (AGGREGATE, parameter(small_building)),
}


def render(name: str) -> Image.Image:
    accent, paint = ICONS[name]
    image = Image.new("RGBA", (CANVAS, CANVAS), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    paint(image, accent)
    return image.resize((SIZE, SIZE), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)


def classes_with_icons() -> set[str]:
    """Concrete classes that show an icon: components, parameters, and the assembly info."""
    pattern = re.compile(r"public sealed class (\w+) : (\w+)")
    names = set()
    for path in SOURCE.rglob("*.cs"):
        if "obj" in path.parts or "bin" in path.parts:
            continue
        for name, _ in pattern.findall(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")):
            if name.endswith(("Component", "Parameter")) or name == "BemGenAssemblyInfo":
                names.add(name)
    return names


def main() -> int:
    expected = classes_with_icons()
    missing = sorted(expected - ICONS.keys())
    unknown = sorted(ICONS.keys() - expected)
    if missing or unknown:
        print(f"Classes without an icon: {missing}; icons without a class: {unknown}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    OUTPUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stale = sorted(p.name for p in OUTPUT.glob("*.png") if p.stem not in ICONS)
    if stale:
        print(f"Icons without a class in {OUTPUT}: {stale}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    for name in sorted(ICONS):
        render(name).save(OUTPUT / f"{name}.png", optimize=False)
    print(f"Wrote {len(ICONS)} icons to {OUTPUT.relative_to(ROOT)}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

- [x] **Step 4: Look at the icons**

Draw a contact sheet of all icons at 8× (nearest neighbour) into the build folder, which git ignores:

```bash
python - <<'EOF'
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image
icons = sorted(Path("src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons").glob("*.png"))
cell = 24 * 8 + 16
sheet = Image.new("RGBA", (6 * cell, -(-len(icons) // 6) * cell), (235, 235, 235, 255))
for i, path in enumerate(icons):
    big = Image.open(path).convert("RGBA").resize((24 * 8, 24 * 8), Image.Resampling.NEAREST)
    sheet.alpha_composite(big, ((i % 6) * cell + 8, (i // 6) * cell + 8))
Path("src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
sheet.save("src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png")
print(len(icons), "icons drawn on src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png")
EOF
```

6. Replace

```markdown
- Linear block: a full-depth stair at the west end (`ST`), a double-loaded corridor (`CO`), and one row of equal dwelling units on each side (`US1…`, `UN1…`); one zone per dwelling unit (D-009). Lengths are metres.

   with

```markdown
- Mechanism (ADR-006): one generator per typology family of the precedent study ([families](../research/precedents/hdhm-typologies.md), D-082, D-083), each a `PlanGenerator` subclass in `Lod.Generators/<Family>/` whose layout returns the footprint and the zones as polygons; the base checks the presets, builds the surfaces, places the windows, and records provenance. Layouts are axis-aligned in the plan frame (origin at the south-west corner, the bar along plan east); *Orientation* turns plan north and *Transform Plan* moves the result. Parameter records of S8 have a static `Default` with the ADR-006 defaults, which the components use as their input defaults. A parameter that would remove one of the family's essential relationships (a count below the family minimum) or a length that is not positive is the error `InvalidParameter`. Lengths are metres.
- Linear block (`HDHM-TYP-009`, double-sided internal corridor bar): a full-depth stair at the west end (`ST`), a double-loaded corridor (`CO`), and one row of equal dwelling units on each side (`US1…`, `UN1…`); one zone per dwelling unit (D-009). Checked against the family's rules in S8.1 and unchanged.
- Stair bay bar (`HDHM-TYP-001`): `BayCount` (≥ 2, default 3) stair bays of dwelling | stair | dwelling through the full depth (`UnitWidth` 7 m, `StairWidth` 3 m, `Depth` 12 m): stairs `ST1…STn`, then dwellings `U1…U2n`, west to east; each stair adjoins the two dwellings of its bay; no corridor.
- Stair pair (`HDHM-TYP-008`): one stair bay as its own building, with the same layout code (`StairBayLayout`): `ST1`, `U1`, `U2`; defaults as the stair bay bar.
- Gallery bar (`HDHM-TYP-002`): `UnitCount` (≥ 2, default 6) dwellings of `UnitWidth` 6 m through `Depth` 10 m between two end stairs of `StairWidth` 4 m: `ST1` (west), `ST2` (east), `U1…Un`. The gallery is not modelled (D-083): no gallery zone or geometry, and the gallery-side façade is an ordinary outdoor façade.
- Terrace row (`HDHM-TYP-006`): `PlotCount` (≥ 2, default 6) attached houses of `PlotWidth` 5.5 m through `Depth` 10 m, `U1…Un` named *House i*, neighbours sharing a dividing wall; one dwelling zone per plot and storey, the house's own stair not zoned; only a *Dwelling Unit* preset.
```

2. Replace

```markdown
Relative comparisons use a floor of 1, `|a − b| ≤ rel · max(1, |a|, |b|)`, so values near zero are compared absolutely.
```

   with

```markdown
Relative comparisons use a floor of 1, `|a − b| ≤ rel · max(1, |a|, |b|)`, so values near zero are compared absolutely.

**`GeometryLimits`** (S8.1, ADR-011) is the only place where the minimum wall and window geometry of the plan generators is defined: `MinimumWindowWallLength` 1 m, `MinimumWindowWidth` 0.3 m, and `MinimumWindowHeight` 0.3 m in `GeometryLimits.Default`. It is an immutable record whose constructor rejects values that are not positive and finite with an `ArgumentOutOfRangeException`. `PlanGenerator` takes it through its constructor (the default when omitted) and records non-default limits in the plan's provenance. An outdoor wall or window below a limit gets no window and the info `WindowOmitted`; the limits do not apply to rebuilt walls (ADR-012).
```

- [x] **Step 4: Edit `docs/architecture/validation.md`**

Make this replacement; the "Replace" text occurs exactly once in the file.

1. Replace

```markdown
- Window positions and sizes. Where walls are rebuilt, each outdoor wall gets one window centred on it with the glazed area of the source windows it covers (D-079, [ADR-012](../decisions/ADR-012-centred-windows.md)), so individual windows are not conserved by design; the rule is covered by the unit tests of `CentredWindows` and the integration tests `CentredWindowTests`. Validation compares the glazed areas in total and per orientation, which the rule conserves per wall. Every window is checked per wall when it is built, not by validation: a source window must lie on one target wall (`WindowNotHosted`) and a wall's glazed area must be smaller than its area (`GlazingExceedsWall`); both hold by construction once the façades are covered (D-041) and are kept as guards.
```

Where walls are rebuilt, each outdoor wall gets one window centred on it with the glazed area of the source windows it covers (D-079, [ADR-012](../decisions/ADR-012-centred-windows.md)), so individual windows are not conserved by design; the rule is covered by the unit tests of `CentredWindows` and the integration tests `CentredWindowTests`. Validation compares the glazed areas in total and per orientation, which the rule conserves per wall. The reference glazing is the plan's: a generator's wall or window below the ADR-011 minimum has no window (`WindowOmitted`), and validation compares with the glazing the generator placed, not with WWR × wall area. Every window is checked per wall when it is built, not by validation: a source window must lie on one target wall (`WindowNotHosted`) and a wall's glazed area must be smaller than its area (`GlazingExceedsWall`); both hold by construction once the façades are covered (D-041) and are kept as guards.
```

### S8.1 checklist — bar typologies (`v0.8.0`)

S8.1 adds four plan generator components in *2 Generate* ([ADR-006](../decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md)) and the minimum wall and window geometry of every generator ([ADR-011](../decisions/ADR-011-minimum-wall-and-window-geometry.md)). Use the three default preset components (*Dwelling Unit Preset*, *Stair Preset*, and for the linear plan *Corridor Preset*) with nothing connected.

#### Toolbar and icons

- [ ] *2 Generate* shows *Linear Plan Generator*, *Stair Bay Bar*, *Stair Pair*, *Gallery Bar*, *Terrace Row*, and *Transform Plan*, each with its own green icon.
- [ ] Inputs, in order, with their defaults: *Stair Bay Bar* (SBar) Bay Count 3, Unit Width 7, Depth 12, Stair Width 3, Floor Height 3, Orientation 0, Dwelling Unit, Stair, Preview Location; *Stair Pair* (SPair) Unit Width 7, Depth 12, Stair Width 3, Floor Height 3, Orientation 0, Dwelling Unit, Stair, Preview Location; *Gallery Bar* (GBar) Unit Count 6, Unit Width 6, Depth 10, Stair Width 4, Floor Height 3, Orientation 0, Dwelling Unit, Stair, Preview Location; *Terrace Row* (Terr) Plot Count 6, Plot Width 5.5, Depth 10, Floor Height 3, Orientation 0, Dwelling Unit, Preview Location. Each has one output, *Plan*.
- [ ] With a preset input unconnected, the component shows Grasshopper's missing-input warning and outputs nothing (D-065).

#### Default plans

1. Place each new generator with its presets connected and nothing else changed; connect *Inspect* to each *Plan*.
   - [ ] *Stair Bay Bar*: no runtime message; nine zones `ST1`–`ST3`, `U1`–`U6`; footprint 51 m × 12 m (612 m²), stairs 36 m², dwellings 84 m²; every stair is flanked by the two dwellings of its bay (`ST2` between `U3` and `U4`, x = 24–27 m); 20 windows, 102.6 m² of glazing (40.5 south, 40.5 north, 10.8 east, 10.8 west in *Validate* on its *No Simplification* floor).
   - [ ] *Stair Pair*: three zones `ST1`, `U1`, `U2`; footprint 17 m × 12 m (204 m²); 8 windows, 48.6 m² of glazing.
   - [ ] *Gallery Bar*: eight zones `ST1`, `ST2`, `U1`–`U6`; footprint 44 m × 10 m (440 m²), stairs 40 m² at both ends, dwellings 60 m²; no gallery in the preview; 18 windows, 75.6 m² of glazing (34.8 south, 34.8 north, 3 east, 3 west).
   - [ ] *Terrace Row*: six zones `U1`–`U6` named *House 1*–*House 6*; footprint 33 m × 10 m (330 m²), 55 m² each; 14 windows, 77.4 m² of glazing.
   - [ ] Each *Inspect* report equals the snapshot `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/<Family>/Snapshots/<family>-canonical.txt`.

#### Combinations

2. Connect each new plan to the four plan simplifiers (*Perimeter Core* Depth 4.57) and each floor three times, Multipliers 1, 3, 1, to the four floor aggregators.
   - [ ] *Semantic Merge* gives 7 zones for *Stair Bay Bar* (the three stairs stay apart; dwellings of neighbouring bays merge), 3 for *Stair Pair*, 3 for *Gallery Bar*, and 1 for *Terrace Row*.
   - [ ] For all 16 combinations of each generator, *Validate* gives Passed = True (only `ConditionedFloorArea` may read `note`), no aggregator shows a warning, and *Convert2BEM* with Override = False converts without an error.

#### Rotated placement

3. Put each new plan through *Transform Plan* with a 30° rotation about the world z axis and a move by 5.3, −2.1, 0, then through each simplifier and each aggregator (Multipliers 1, 3, 1).
   - [ ] *Validate* gives Passed = True for all 16 combinations of each generator, with the same total glazing as the unrotated plan.

#### Parameters and the minimum geometry

4. Change inputs one at a time and set each back afterwards.
   - [ ] *Stair Bay Bar* Bay Count 1: `Error InvalidParameter: BayCount must be at least 2 (one bay is HDHM-TYP-008, Stair Pair), got 1.` and no plan. *Gallery Bar* Unit Count 1 and *Terrace Row* Plot Count 1 give the matching errors; a width or depth of 0 gives `… must be positive, got 0.`
   - [ ] A *Stair Preset* in the *Dwelling Unit* input of *Stair Bay Bar*: `Error PresetSpaceTypeMismatch [DwellingUnit]: Input DwellingUnit needs a DwellingUnit preset, got 'Example Stair' (Stair).`
   - [ ] *Terrace Row* Plot Width 0.8: the plan is produced with twelve remarks such as `Info WindowOmitted [U1/W1]: Outdoor wall U1/W1 is 0.8 m long, below the 1 m minimum for a window (ADR-011); it has no window (0.72 m² of glazing not placed).`; only the two 10 m end walls show windows, and *Validate* on its *No Simplification* floor passes.
   - [ ] *Linear Plan Generator* with its defaults shows no remark, and its *Inspect* report equals `linear-plan-canonical.txt` (ADR-011 changes no existing plan).

Wherever zones are merged and walls are rebuilt (`SemanticMerge`, `PerimeterCore`, `SingleZonePerFloor`, `SingleZonePerFloorType`, `SingleZoneBuilding`), every outdoor wall carries one centred window with the glazed area of the source windows it covers (`CentredWindows`, D-079, [ADR-012](docs/decisions/ADR-012-centred-windows.md)); the other steps keep their input's windows. ADR-006, ADR-011, ADR-012, ADR-013, and the S4.2, S5, and S8.1 plans were accepted by the controller and await the owner's reading (D-075, D-080, D-084). ```markdown
### D-086 — S8.1 complete

- **Date:** <date> · **Decided by:** controller (unsupervised run, D-084) · **Status:** Provisional
- Sub-stage S8.1 (D-084) is complete and tagged `v0.8.0` locally; pushing `main` and the tag waits for the owner (D-084). [ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md) and [ADR-011](ADR-011-minimum-wall-and-window-geometry.md), accepted by the controller under D-084 and pending the owner's reading, fix the plan generation mechanism (one `PlanGenerator` subclass per *High-Density Housing* family with typed parameter and preset records; design defaults, zone IDs, and realisation of the rules for all nine families, D-083) and the minimum wall and window geometry of every generator. The precedent page `docs/research/precedents/hdhm-typologies.md` paraphrases the nine families with their stable IDs and the pinned source of D-082. ADR-002's placement rule is refined by ADR-011, and the research brief §10 states the exception.
- `GeometryLimits` in `Lod.Core.Common` (`MinimumWindowWallLength` 1 m, `MinimumWindowWidth` 0.3 m, `MinimumWindowHeight` 0.3 m) is passed through the `PlanGenerator` constructor, the defaults when omitted. An outdoor wall or window below a limit by more than the distance tolerance gets no window and the new info `WindowOmitted`; only non-default limits are recorded in provenance. `LayoutMeasures.PlaceWindowsByRatio` takes the limits and tolerances and returns a `Result`. Rebuilt walls are not subject to the limits (ADR-012).
- `LinearPlanGenerator` realises `HDHM-TYP-009` and is unchanged apart from its documentation. New in `Lod.Generators`: `StairBayBarGenerator` (001, at least 2 bays), `StairPairGenerator` (008, one bay of the shared `StairBayLayout`), `GalleryBarGenerator` (002, at least 2 dwellings, the gallery not modelled, D-083), and `TerraceRowGenerator` (006, at least 2 plots, one dwelling zone per plot and storey, D-085), each with a static `Default` parameter record. New components in *2 Generate*, with icons: *Stair Bay Bar* (SBar, `911c3204-42bb-4023-bdb7-2931ea1caad3`), *Stair Pair* (SPair, `5669d39b-45e6-47ea-9250-f2304d005335`), *Gallery Bar* (GBar, `a58df462-b3c2-46b7-be3d-7ea9f4629280`), and *Terrace Row* (Terr, `f694e595-dba2-4b98-9d1e-f1492e3edf39`). *Linear Plan Generator*'s description names `HDHM-TYP-009`; its inputs and GUID are unchanged.
- The eight existing snapshots are byte-identical; four canonical snapshots are new. 152 core, 91 generator, and 319 integration tests pass on `net8.0`, including every bar family with every simplifier and aggregator on its canonical plan and on the plan rotated by 131.4° and moved by (15.08, −153.9) m.
- The S8.1 smoke test is recorded in the close-out commit; items it did not perform stay open for a person (D-058).
- S8.2 (families 007, 003, and 004) can start; S6 (Convert2IDF) is unaffected.
```

### Task 18: Fresh-clone gate and push (owner; not performed in this run, D-084)

**Files:** none

**Not performed in this run.** D-084 leaves pushing to the owner: the controller does not run this task, and its steps stay unticked when the stage is closed. They are listed so that the owner, or the controller once the owner authorises it, pushes the stage the way S5 was pushed (D-080); never a force push. `v0.4.2` and `v0.5.0` are not part of this task.

- [ ] **Step 1: Check the state to push**

```bash
git status --short
git fetch origin
git rev-parse main v0.8.0^{commit}
git merge-base --is-ancestor origin/main main && echo fast-forward
git tag --list "v0.8.0"
```

Expected: no output from `git status --short`; the two SHAs are equal (the tag points at the tip of `main`, unless later commits were added on purpose); `fast-forward` (local `main` contains `origin/main`); the tag. If `origin/main` has commits that local `main` does not, stop and report: nothing is pushed, and no rebase or force push is attempted.

- [ ] **Step 2: Verify a fresh clone of the tag**

```bash
git clone --branch v0.8.0 . ../BEMGen-v0.8.0-clone
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File ../BEMGen-v0.8.0-clone/scripts/verify.ps1
git -C ../BEMGen-v0.8.0-clone describe --tags
```

Expected: the clone reports a detached `HEAD` at `v0.8.0`; verify builds the clone with `0 Warning(s)` and `0 Error(s)` and ends with `VERIFY PASSED`, with 152 core, 91 generators, and 319 integration tests passed on net8.0; `describe` prints `v0.8.0`. Then delete the clone (`rm -rf ../BEMGen-v0.8.0-clone` in Git Bash). If verify fails, stop: nothing is pushed.

- [ ] **Step 3: Push (no force)**

```bash
git push origin main
git push origin v0.8.0
git ls-remote --tags origin v0.8.0
git status -sb
```

Expected: each push succeeds without `--force` (`main` as a fast-forward, the tag as `[new tag]`); `ls-remote` lists the tag (with its peeled `^{}` line); `git status -sb` shows `## main...origin/main` with nothing ahead or behind. A rejected push is not retried with force: stop and report.

## Exit criteria

- The precedent page paraphrases the nine families with their IDs and the pinned source (D-082) and holds no book content; ADR-006 and ADR-011 exist and are accepted by the controller under D-084 (pending the owner's reading); ADR-002's status line points to ADR-011; the research brief §10 states the exception.
- `GeometryLimits` (1 m, 0.3 m, 0.3 m) is the only place for the minimum wall and window geometry; a generator's outdoor wall or window below a limit gets no window and the info `WindowOmitted`; non-default limits are recorded in provenance (`GeometryLimitsTests`, six tests; `PlanGeneratorTests`, four new tests).
- `StairBayBarGenerator` (001), `StairPairGenerator` (008, on the shared `StairBayLayout`), `GalleryBarGenerator` (002, no gallery), and `TerraceRowGenerator` (006, one zone per plot and storey) implement ADR-006 with its defaults, zone IDs, and validation; each has tiling, window, relationship, invalid-parameter, determinism, provenance, and snapshot tests; `LinearPlanGenerator` is checked as `HDHM-TYP-009` and unchanged.
- Every new family validates with every plan simplifier and floor aggregator, on its canonical plan and rotated and moved (`BarTypologyTests`, 132 cases).
- Snapshots: the eight existing snapshots byte-identical; four new canonical snapshots.
- Four new components in *2 Generate* with the GUIDs of this plan, their ADR-006 defaults, required preset inputs, *Preview Location* last, and icons (32 icons); no existing component, input, output, or GUID changed; *Linear Plan Generator*'s description names `HDHM-TYP-009`.
- `scripts/verify.ps1` prints `VERIFY PASSED`; tests: 152 core, 91 generators, 319 integration on `net8.0`; 0 warnings.
- The S8.1 smoke test is recorded in the close-out commit, with the items left for a person named.
- Version 0.8.0, local tag `v0.8.0`; AGENTS.md, README, the roadmap Progress line, detailed-plans heading and row, and layout, and the decision-log entry D-086 are written; this plan is ticked and marked Done.
- Nothing is pushed in this run (D-084); Task 18 is left for the owner.

## Notes for the reviewer

1. **Source.** Every code block, snapshot, and new document was extracted programmatically from a verified reference implementation built on `main` at `baddbb7` (D-082 to D-084), 16 commits; local `main` is `22e90fd`, which adds only the D-085 entry to the decision log, so every file the reference touches is identical on both. The reference's commit order is kept with three exceptions: its roadmap commit is split into the stage edits committed with this plan and the layout lines of the close-out (Task 17), as for S5; its component commit is split into Task 11 (the components) and Task 13 (the icons and `make_icons.py`), as S4.2 did with Tasks 12 and 16; and Task 9 holds two reference commits (the check, then the doc comment). Every task was then replayed on a clone of `22e90fd` from this plan's file contents and replacements: the red errors of Tasks 4–8 (with line and column numbers and in the compiler's order), the single failing snapshot test of Tasks 5–8 and the four received snapshots (byte-identical to the expected contents), the failure of `make_icons.py` before Task 13 and the 32 hashes after it, the counts after every task, a 0-warning build after every task, every documentation replacement (each "Replace" text occurs exactly once when it is applied, and the result equals the reference), the close-out edits, and `VERIFY PASSED` on a fresh clone of the replayed `v0.8.0` are observed results. The replayed end state equals the reference apart from line endings, the version, the decision log (D-085 on `main`, D-086 from the close-out), and the roadmap's Progress line and detailed-plans heading and row.
2. **Slices and branches.** The roadmap names no slices for S8.1; this plan uses five slices and a close-out, named after D-004: `docs/decisions-generation-mechanism` (the name the roadmap's original S7 slice 3 gave ADR-006), `feature/generators-geometry-limits`, `feature/generators-bar-typologies`, `feature/grasshopper-bar-typologies`, `docs/architecture-bar-typologies`, and `chore/repo-s8.1-close-out`. Merges rebase onto local `main`. Unlike S5, this run ends without a push (D-084); Task 18 is written out for the owner and stays unticked.
3. **D-085 and ADR-006.** D-085 (a dwelling that spans storeys is one zone per storey) was logged on `main` after the reference's ADR-006 text was settled. ADR-006's `HDHM-TYP-006` section records the same rule ("each plot is one dwelling zone per storey"; the house's stair inside the dwelling and not zoned) and calls it a consequence of the plan → floor → building pipeline that D-009 "does not cover", while D-085 says D-009 applies per storey; the rule is the same, the wording differs, and neither ADR-006's decision line nor `TerraceRowGenerator`'s summary cites D-085. The plan keeps the reference text unchanged; the close-out cites D-085 in AGENTS.md, the roadmap Progress line, and D-086. Citing D-085 in ADR-006 is a one-line follow-up for the owner's reading.
4. **`HDHM-TYP-007` stays in S8.2.** As specified in ADR-006, the point-access plate is a square (22 m × 22 m by default) with four dwellings in a pinwheel around a central `Stair` core that has no outdoor wall. Its footprint is a rectangle without holes, so it could in principle be generated now, but it is the first family with an interior zone and the first that is not a bar; D-084 assigns it to S8.2 with 003 and 004, and S8.1 implements only its specification.
5. **Provenance order.** `Provenance.Of` sorts parameters by key, so every provenance test lists keys alphabetically (`BayCount`, `Depth`, `FloorHeight`, …) and `OnlyNonDefaultLimitsAreRecordedInProvenance` expects `MinimumWindowHeight`, `MinimumWindowWallLength`, `MinimumWindowWidth` in that order. While the reference was built, the first version of that test expected insertion order; the expectation was corrected to the sorted order, and no check was weakened.
6. **ADR-011 does not apply to rebuilt walls.** The limits apply only where a generator places windows (the base `PlanGenerator`). Wherever walls are rebuilt, ADR-012 still gives every target outdoor wall one centred window with the glazed area it covers, whatever its size, because glazed area is an invariant (GLOBAL.md scientific rule 1); such a window may be smaller than the minimum size and is neither omitted nor reported. With the current zoning strategies a glazed target wall contains at least one whole glazed source wall and is therefore at least 1 m long. `WindowNotHosted` and `GlazingExceedsWall` stay guards; a strategy that cuts a source wall inside a window must come with its own ADR.
7. **`PlaceWindowsByRatio` changes its signature.** The public `LayoutMeasures.PlaceWindowsByRatio` now takes the limits and tolerances and returns a `Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>>`; its only callers are `PlanGenerator` and one test (`LayoutSurfaceBuilderTests.WindowsAreSizedByEachZonesRatio`), whose assertions are unchanged. No backward compatibility is kept (D-064).
8. **Tasks without a red phase.** Tasks 9 and 10 add verification tests only: D-083 asks that the linear plan be checked against `HDHM-TYP-009` and kept, and D-084 states that bars need no pipeline change. Both checks passed at once in the reference and in the replay; each task says to stop if one fails, since a failure would need a decision, not a fix in the test. The build-error red phases of Tasks 4–8 list only declaration-level errors: the compiler stops before binding method bodies, so Task 4 reports one error and Tasks 5–8 four or five.
9. **Defaults live in one place, except for the linear plan.** ADR-006 gives every S8 parameter record a static `Default` that the components use. `LinearPlanParameters` (S2) has none; its component defaults stay in `LinearPlanGeneratorComponent`, unchanged, as ADR-006's `HDHM-TYP-009` section says.
10. **Documents changed beyond the design brief.** The research brief §10 gets one sentence on the ADR-011 exception (Task 3); ADR-002's status line points to ADR-011; `pipeline.md`'s heading becomes "Grasshopper components through S8.1" (with the link in `program-presets.md`) and "Not in Checkpoint 1" becomes "Not implemented yet", whose S7 and S8 rows become S8.2 and S8.3 rows; `domain-model.md` and `validation.md` describe `GeometryLimits` and the plan's actual glazing as the reference; the roadmap's detailed-plans heading drops "(Checkpoint 1)" at the close-out; README's layout line names the typology families. The repository spec (`docs/LOD_grasshopper_plugin_repository_spec.md`) is not edited.
11. **Research brief §4 unchanged.** §4 still lists the linear, point, and courtyard blocks as the minimum typologies. The *High-Density Housing* families realise and extend that list (009 and the bars are linear blocks, 007 a point block, 004 and 005 courtyards); §4 is not an equivalence rule, so GLOBAL.md scientific rule 7 does not require a change, and the owner may align it with D-082 later.
12. **A fix on `main` between S5 and S8.1.** `4b07283` (merging coplanar faces of zone volumes in `RhinoGeometry.cs`) landed on `main` after `v0.5.0`. The S8.1 reference is built on top of it and holds that file in its `main` version; it is not an S8.1 file and no S8.1 task changes it.
13. **Icons.** The 28 existing PNGs are byte-identical after Task 13 (Pillow 12.3.0); the four new ones were compared with the reference's bytes in the replay. The contact-sheet descriptions are from the replay's sheet.
14. **Smoke-test values.** The expected values of Task 16 were observed in one scripted headless Rhino 8.25 session (D-056) on the reference's last commit: registration and inputs, the default plans (bounding boxes, footprint, provenance, *Boundaries*, *Windows*), the 64 canonical and 64 rotated combinations (states and *Validate* Passed), and the three parameter scenarios. *Inspect* texts, *Validate* line values, *Convert2BEM* on the combinations, and the viewport were not observed there.
15. **Clean working tree.** The `git status` expectations assume a working tree with no untracked files outside `.gitignore` before Task 1.
16. **`check_plans.py` false positives.** The Global Constraints line that states the no-attribution rule is flagged as a possible attribution; it states the rule. The `make_icons.py` docstring line that names the Pillow version of the icons is flagged, as in S4.1 and S4.2.
